The outlook for growth in Australia’s
major trading partners (MTPs) has
been revised higher over the next
couple of years, but growth is
expected to moderate from its
current strong pace.
The MTP GDP growth forecast has been revised
upwards to 4.0 per cent in 2026, before slowing to
3.5 per cent in 2027 and 3.4 per cent in 2028 as
growth in global AI-related activity and China is
expected to moderate (Graph 3.2). Growth has been
above some estimates of potential and stronger than

expected over the past year, reflecting strong AI-related
activity and, in some economies, continued fiscal
support. These have more than offset the headwinds
from the Middle East conflict. MTP growth is expected to
moderate to around potential over 2027 and 2028.
Overall, we judge that the risks around this outlook for
MTP growth are broadly balanced. The expansion in
global AI-related investment and production may be
more persistent than Consensus forecasters have

factored in, but higher energy prices could also weigh
on activity by more than currently assumed. The outlook
for MTP growth as a whole is broadly in line with the
International Monetary Fund’s projections in the October
2026 World Economic Outlook.
The upward revision to MTP growth is concentrated
in high-income east Asian economies, which are key
exporters of AI-related goods. Strong demand for
semiconductors and other AI-enabling technologies has

supported activity in economies that are deeply
integrated into regional technology supply chains, such
as South Korea and Taiwan. While the outlook for growth
in 2027 has been revised a little higher by external
forecasters, it is still expected to moderate from the
strong pace over the past year towards estimates of
potential growth.

Growth in other advanced economies has also been
more resilient than expected, prompting upward

revisions to near-term forecasts. AI-related investment
and stronger underlying momentum have supported
activity in the United States, while defence spending and
broader resilience across services and investment have
contributed to growth in the euro area. Activity in the
United Kingdom has also held up better than expected,
supported by business investment. These tailwinds
across advanced economies have so far offset some of
the drag to activity from higher energy costs. However,

external forecasters and central banks expect
persistently elevated energy costs to reduce household
purchasing power, raise firms’ costs and increasingly
weigh on consumption and investment. In some
advanced economies, tighter financial conditions will
also weigh on growth.
3.2 The global outlook
Chapter 3  |  OutlookOutlookOutlookOutlookOutlookOutlookOutlook
Reserve Bank of Australia  |  Statement on Monetary Policy  |  November 2026Statement on Monetary Policy  |  November 2026Statement on Monetary Policy  |  November 2026Statement on Monetary Policy  |  November 2026Statement on Monetary Policy  |  November 2026Statement on Monetary Policy  |  November 2026Statement on Monetary Policy  |  November 2026 66

### Page 70

The outlook for GDP growth in China is broadly
unchanged from the August Statement, though
there have been some changes to the expected
timing and composition of growth. GDP growth is
forecast to be 4.6 per cent in 2026, before easing slightly
to 4.5 per cent in 2027 and 4.4 per cent in 2028. The
pick-up in growth in the September quarter is expected
to continue in the near term, given the announcement
of new policy support for infrastructure investment and

signals from authorities that supporting economic
activity will be elevated as a near-term policy priority.
Infrastructure investment is now expected to recover
from late 2026 as this support takes effect, while
structural headwinds are expected to continue weighing
on domestic demand more broadly. The timing and
effectiveness of the policy support remain uncertain,
particularly given high local government debt and a
range of barriers to some local governments

commencing infrastructure projects.
Longer-term government bond yields have risen
across advanced economies, alongside resilient
activity and persistent inflation. The increase appears
to reflect a combination of higher expected near-term
policy rates and higher longer-term real rates, reflecting
a mix of higher term premia and potentially higher
neutral rates. Market-based measures of long-term
inflation expectations remain consistent with central

bank targets. Strong investment and public demand
may be placing upward pressure on real rates, and
expectations of stronger future productivity growth –
including from the adoption of AI – may also be
contributing. Higher public and private debt issuance
and uncertainty about inflation and fiscal trajectories
may also be adding to the compensation investors
require to hold longer-duration bonds (see Box A: How
Does an Increase in Longer-term Real Yields Affect

Financial Conditions? and Box B: Developments in the
Neutral Rate of Interest).
The global inflation outlook has been revised higher,
reflecting an increase in energy prices and
continued resilience in global demand. Consensus
forecasters expect headline inflation to remain above
central bank targets into 2027 in many advanced
economies. Services disinflation has stalled, while core
goods inflation has picked up. The outlook for globally
traded goods inflation has been revised higher,

reflecting higher commodity prices and further strong
increases in export prices for semiconductors and other
AI-related goods across parts of Asia. AI-related price
pressures have not broadened materially beyond
electronics but could become more persistent or spread
to other parts of global supply chains if strong demand
continues to run ahead of the expansion in productive
capacity (see Key risk #2). The further increase in energy
prices since August could add to these upstream cost

pressures, although the extent and timing of
pass-through to consumer prices remains uncertain.
Overall, we continue to judge that the risks to global
inflation remain skewed to the upside.

### Page 71

Australian GDP growth is expected
to remain below our estimate of
potential growth throughout the
forecast period.
GDP growth has slowed since the end of 2025 and is
expected to remain subdued over the next couple of
years (Graph 3.3). The expected slowing in growth over
2026 reflects the effects of several headwinds weighing
on private activity, including the reduction in household
disposable incomes from higher inflation, weaker
conditions in the established housing market, and the

tightening in monetary policy, which more than offset
very strong data centre-related investment. Growth is
expected to remain below potential over the forecast
period, reflecting an assumption that financial
conditions remain restrictive (see Key judgement #2).
Consistent with the latest state and federal budgets,
public demand is expected to continue supporting
growth over the forecast period.
The risks to activity appear to be broadly balanced.

Stronger-than-expected global growth and further
strength in AI and data centre-related activity pose
upside risks to domestic growth (see Key risk #2).
However, historically low consumer and business
sentiment could be pointing to the risk that the
combined effect on domestic activity of the Middle East
conflict, the decline in housing prices and the tightening
in monetary policy could be more than assumed (see
Key risk #3).
The level of GDP at end-2028 is expected to be

broadly unchanged from the August forecasts.
Growth is projected to be slightly stronger than
expected in the near term, reflecting a much stronger
outlook for data centre-related investment compared
with the August Statement. Weighing against that is the
higher assumed market path for the cash rate, a weaker
outlook for the housing market and higher oil prices.
These pull growth in the latter part of the forecast period
a little below that expected in the August Statement.

Business investment is expected to increase strongly
and the outlook has been revised up significantly,

largely driven by data centre-related investment.
Business investment was stronger than expected in the
June quarter and firms’ surveyed expectations of future
investment were again revised higher. External
projections for the expected increase in Australia’s data
centre capacity through to 2030 have also continued to
be revised up as the size of the development pipeline
has increased. Our forecast for data centre investment is
in line with recent baseline external projections, but

below projections that assume Australia becomes a
significant regional hub for data centres. There is
considerable uncertainty around the extent of data
centre investment over coming years. This will depend
on a range of factors such as government policy,
community expectations, future demand for AI services
and grid capacity. We continue to expect that much of
this additional investment will be imported (around
70 per cent), limiting the boost to GDP growth. However,

data centre construction, as well as any potential
spillovers to higher investment in energy and utilities
infrastructure, will require domestically sourced labour
and materials. At this stage, most of this investment
pipeline is in New South Wales and Victoria where liaison
information suggests capacity constraints in the
construction sector are somewhat less binding.
3.3 Domestic outlook

### Page 72

At the same time, a higher assumed cash rate path, a
more significant easing in the established housing
market and higher global oil prices are expected to
weigh more on activity than in the August forecasts.
The forecasts are conditioned on a higher market path
for interest rates relative to August. In addition,
established housing prices have continued to fall more
quickly than expected, with external forecasters now
expecting a deeper downturn than assumed a few

months ago. The outlook for oil prices is also much
higher than assumed in August, which will weigh on real
household incomes. Reflecting these factors, the outlook
for household consumption and dwelling investment
has been revised lower, as has the outlook for business
investment unrelated to data centres.
The labour market is expected to
continue easing over the forecast
period, consistent with
below-potential GDP growth.
The unemployment rate is forecast to increase to

5.1 per cent by the end of 2028, which is around
1⁄4 percentage point higher than previously
expected (Graph 3.4). The unemployment rate has risen
by more than expected over recent months, and the
upward revision to forecast relative to the August
Statement largely reflects this higher starting point.
Elevated cost-of-living pressures appear to have
encouraged workers to enter or remain in the labour
force to a greater extent in recent months than was

expected at the time of the August Statement. These
pressures are expected to continue to support
participation for some time. The participation rate is
expected to decline very modestly by the end of 2028 as
easing cyclical pressures are expected to more than
offset the longer-run upward trend in female
participation. A slightly weaker outlook for demand later
in the forecast period is also contributing a little to the
upward revision to the unemployment rate profile by

late 2028.

As in previous forecasts, the
employment-to-population ratio is expected to
decline through to late 2028. Employment growth is

expected to remain broadly stable over the next year, in
line with the leading indicators of labour demand. From
mid-2027, employment growth is expected to be a little
weaker than in the August Statement, reflecting the
weaker outlook for GDP growth later in the forecast
period. The forecast employment-to-population ratio is
similar to the August Statement in the near term, before
declining more quickly to reach a lower level by the end
of the forecast period.

Reserve Bank of Australia  |  Statement on Monetary Policy  |  November 2026Statement on Monetary Policy  |  November 2026Statement on Monetary Policy  |  November 2026Statement on Monetary Policy  |  November 2026Statement on Monetary Policy  |  November 2026Statement on Monetary Policy  |  November 2026Statement on Monetary Policy  |  November 2026 69

### Page 73

The economy is expected to move
into balance in 2027, with spare
capacity then emerging in the
economy over the rest of the forecast
period.
Below-potential output growth and an easing in
labour market conditions are expected to see the
economy move from a position of excess demand to
around balance in 2027, before some spare capacity
emerges. The timing of the return to balance is slightly
later than in the August Statement, as a result of the
upgrade to near-term GDP growth.

However, there is considerable uncertainty around
the assessment of when the economy will return to
balance and the extent of spare capacity that
emerges. The impact of conflict-related costs on
inflation over the period ahead will also make it more
difficult to assess the degree of underlying capacity
pressures in the domestic economy. Our assumption for
potential output growth over the next couple of years
assumes productivity growth will pick up gradually (see

Key risk #4). However, with the level of labour
productivity remaining around the same rate as prior to
the pandemic – implying near-zero productivity growth
on average over that period – continued weak
productivity outcomes may be constraining the ability of
firms to grow their supply capacity without generating
inflationary pressure.
Wages growth is expected to pick up
a little in the near term, then
moderate gradually from 2027 as
labour market conditions ease.

The forecast for year-ended wages growth has been
revised a little higher across the forecast period. In
the near term, nominal wages growth is expected to be
supported by the effect of higher-than-expected
capacity pressures in the first half of the year as well as
the Fair Work Commission’s award wage decision (which
had already been incorporated in the August forecasts).
The influence of both higher-than-expected inflation
outcomes and inflation expectations on wage

negotiations is expected to add further upward pressure
on wages growth relative to the August forecast. Some
large wage increases in enterprise bargaining
agreements are expected to support wages growth in
the near term, although the timing of pay increases for
these agreements may contribute to volatility in
quarterly outcomes, particularly for the public sector.
Wages growth expectations from firms in the RBA’s
liaison program are elevated and suggest little

easing over the year ahead (see Box C: Insights from
Liaison). This is consistent with the central forecast for
wages growth. Growth is expected to moderate from
mid-2027 onwards, reflecting easing labour market
conditions and lower inflation outcomes. Wages growth
is expected to have eased to 2.9 per cent in year-ended
terms by end-2028 (Graph 3.5).

Unit labour cost growth is forecast to
be elevated in the near term, before
easing over the second half of the
forecast period.
Year-ended growth in unit labour costs (ULCs) is
expected to increase in coming quarters, reflecting
weak productivity growth and higher wages growth.
Year-ended growth in ULCs is then expected to
moderate from late 2027 onwards, alongside slowing
nominal wages growth and the pick-up in productivity
growth to its assumed medium-term trend growth rate

of 0.7 per cent per year (Graph 3.6). Nominal ULCs are
the measure of labour costs most relevant for firms’ cost
of production and so for inflation outcomes. Year-ended
ULCs growth is expected to remain above 2.5 per cent –
the rate that is consistent with inflation being
sustainably at target – until late in the forecast period.

The forecast for underlying inflation
has been revised higher over the
next year, mainly owing to higher
conflict-related cost pressures and
higher-than-expected domestic

Trimmed mean inflation is expected to remain
above 3 per cent until the end of 2027, easing
gradually to reach 21⁄2 per cent around mid-2028
(Graph 3.7). Year-ended underlying inflation is expected

to remain elevated at a little above 31⁄2 per cent until
mid-2027, reflecting existing capacity pressures in the
economy and the pass-through of higher

underlying inflation over the coming year or so. From
mid-2027 onwards, inflation is expected to ease due to
the emergence of spare capacity in the economy and

to unwind.

Cost pressures related to the Middle East conflict are
expected to place more upward pressure on
underlying inflation over the year ahead than
previously assumed. A possible re-escalation in the
conflict had been flagged as an upside risk to inflation in
the August Statement. Since then, oil and fuel prices
have increased by much more than had been assumed
and are now assumed to remain at a higher level over
the forecast period. We expect higher global energy

prices will contribute to higher domestic inflationary
pressures in late 2026 and early 2027, even as domestic
activity slows and the labour market eases (Graph 3.8).
The judgement around the degree of pass-through from
global energy prices to domestic prices is broadly similar
to what had been assumed in August. It is consistent
with information from liaison and the Business Outlook
Scenario Survey (BOSS) in September, where firms
reported plans to continue raising prices in response to

past cost increases arising partially from the conflict.
From mid-2027 onwards, we expect cost effects to
unwind in net terms, albeit to a lesser extent than
assumed in the August Statement as oil prices are now
assumed to remain at a higher level than previously
expected and refined margins are assumed to remain
elevated for longer (see Key judgement #1). The balance
of risks to conflict-related inflationary pressures remains
skewed to the upside, particularly as crude oil and

refined fuel prices could remain elevated or rise further
(see Key risk #1).
Relative to the August forecasts, domestic capacity
pressures are also expected to exert a bit more
upward pressure on inflation over the year ahead.
We now judge that stronger-than-expected domestic
capacity pressures explain more of the recent
unexpected strength in underlying inflation (see
Chapter 2: Economic Conditions). Those higher capacity
pressures are also expected to contribute a little more to

inflation over the year ahead. This upward pressure is
expected to ease gradually over the forecast period, as
spare capacity emerges in the economy and the
labour market.

Elevated inflation expectations are expected to

contribute to inflationary pressure over the forecast
period, supporting the pass-through of firms’ costs
to consumer prices. Several measures of short- and
medium-term inflation expectations have increased
alongside the recent increases in fuel prices, and some
measures of longer-term expectations have also drifted
higher (see Chapter 2: Economic Conditions). We judge
that elevated expectations will contribute to higher
realised inflation by supporting expected cost growth,

making firms more willing to pass these higher costs
through to prices, and placing upward pressure on
wages growth. If the conflict in the Middle East is more
protracted than assumed in our baseline forecasts, there
is a risk that inflation expectations may increase further
(see Key risk #1).

### Page 76

Headline inflation has been revised
higher in the near term due to
stronger growth in fuel prices and
higher underlying inflation.
Headline inflation is expected to remain above 3 per
cent until late 2027, which is around three quarters
longer than in the August forecast, and reach the
midpoint of the 2–3 per cent inflation target range a
bit later than expected in August (Graph 3.9). Headline
inflation is expected to peak at 4.5 per cent in the
December quarter this year due to

higher-than-expected fuel prices. The assumed decline
in global oil prices is expected to put downward
pressure on headline inflation from early 2027. Over the
remainder of the forecast period, headline inflation has
been revised higher relative to our August forecasts,
reflecting higher underlying inflation (Graph 3.10).
Headline inflation is expected to reach the midpoint of
the target in mid-2028 as capacity pressures and
conflict-related costs ease.

### Page 77

The staff assess that the risks to the domestic inflation
outlook are skewed to the upside, despite becoming
more balanced since the August Statement as some of
the risks identified then have been incorporated into the
central forecast. In August, we identified three main
sources of upside risk to the global and domestic
inflation outlook: a re-escalation of the Middle East
conflict; domestic capacity pressures being greater than
assumed; and the global AI boom generating more of an

inflationary impetus than assumed. Since then, the risk
of a re-escalation of the Middle East conflict has
crystallised and staff have re-evaluated the extent of
domestic capacity constraints; both of which have led to
upward revisions to the central inflation forecast over the
next year. However, there is still uncertainty around the
outlook for global energy prices, the role of inflation
expectations in price and wage dynamics as well as the
impact on inflation from AI activity.

The staff also identified in August that there were upside
risks to global and domestic economic activity from
AI-related demand, but that domestic economic activity
could be weaker if the Middle East conflict and the
decline in housing prices affected the economy by more
than assumed in the central forecast. These remain key
risks to the outlook.
Key risk #1 – High inflation could be
more persistent if energy prices
remain elevated, or if inflation

expectations increase or start to
have a greater bearing on wage- and
price-setting.
We have modelled a scenario where crude oil
remains around US$100 per barrel and refined
product prices remain at elevated levels over the
forecast period. Many external forecasters are revising
higher their outlook for crude oil and refined product
prices, on concerns that some of the key margins of
adjustment in global energy markets that have helped
to cushion the impact of the disruption to oil supply and

global activity are reaching their limits (see Chapter 2:
Economic Conditions and Key judgement #1).
Persistently higher global oil prices would leave
domestic inflation higher for a longer period than in
the central forecast. Higher oil prices would directly
increase fuel prices and firms’ input costs, with the latter
gradually passed through to consumer prices as firms
adjust to persistently higher costs. By contrast, the
central forecast assumes a decline in oil prices, which

starts to ease cost pressures related to the Middle East
conflict from mid-2027, contributing to lower inflation
over time (Graph 3.11). Higher inflation also raises
inflation expectations in the scenario, reinforcing
inflationary pressures over the forecast period. Assuming
that the cash rate and exchange rate follow the same
path that underpins the central forecasts, underlying
inflation would be around 23⁄4 per cent by
end-2028 rather than being around the 21⁄2 per cent.

3.4 Key risks to the outlook
Chapter 3  |  OutlookOutlookOutlookOutlookOutlookOutlookOutlook
Reserve Bank of Australia  |  Statement on Monetary Policy  |  November 2026Statement on Monetary Policy  |  November 2026Statement on Monetary Policy  |  November 2026Statement on Monetary Policy  |  November 2026Statement on Monetary Policy  |  November 2026Statement on Monetary Policy  |  November 2026Statement on Monetary Policy  |  November 2026 74

### Page 78

It is possible that inflation expectations could rise
even further or that inflation expectations provide
greater impetus to wages growth and inflation
through second-round effects. The central forecasts
and scenario assume that the increase in households’
and firms’ inflation expectations do exert some upward
pressure on inflation and wages growth. However, given
households and firms are paying particularly close
attention to inflation, it is possible that inflation

expectations will influence price- and wage-setting
decisions by more than assumed. This heightened
attentiveness to inflation could also make expectations
more persistent and more responsive to realised
inflation. With headline inflation forecast to rise further in
the near term, this increases the risk that inflation
expectations remain higher for longer than assumed in
our central forecast.
Key risk #2 – The global AI demand
boom may continue to drive up

global growth and inflation.
MTP growth may not moderate as expected over the
next few years if AI-demand growth continues at the
same pace. It is possible that growth forecasts could
continue to be upwardly revised by Consensus
forecasters if the current upswing in AI-related
investment (and spillovers associated with the boom) is
more persistent than currently assumed. The rise in
interest rates across the yield curve in many advanced
economies may reflect higher neutral rates – that is,

expectations that demand will prove more resilient and
inflation more persistent than currently assumed. Higher
longer-term real rates may partly reflect expectations of
stronger future productivity growth, including from the
adoption of AI. Further investment in anticipation of
higher productivity could also support demand and add
to capacity pressures over the next few years, while most
of the productivity gains are likely to manifest beyond
the next two years (at least in Australia).

The ramp-up of investment in data centres may also
contribute to capacity and pricing pressures
domestically, particularly by drawing on resources in
the construction industry. These price pressures could
be stronger than currently expected as the forecast
pipeline of investment is worked through, or if
investment proves stronger than expected.
Key risk #3 – Domestic economic
activity could be weaker than
expected.
It is possible that the headwinds affecting

households and businesses over the next year could
have a larger adverse effect than assumed. The
central forecasts assume that higher oil prices, higher
interest rates and lower housing prices all weigh on
domestic economic activity broadly in line with average
historical relationships. Timely indicators of spending
remain consistent with this assumption, with no
evidence of a material slowing in consumption growth
in recent months. However, the very weak levels of

consumer and business sentiment could be pointing to
a more pronounced slowing in household and business
spending growth than assumed.
We have calibrated a downside domestic activity
scenario where there is a larger response in
household and business spending to these
headwinds. There is a range of estimates for the effects
of these headwinds in the literature, highlighting the
uncertainty around their impact on economic activity.
Drawing on this evidence, the scenario uses larger

responses than assumed in the central forecast, thereby
identifying a plausible downside outlook for domestic
activity. In the scenario, firms feel forced to pass on the
conflict-related costs to prices as in the central forecast;
but households respond more cautiously to the
resultant pressures on real income and wealth, reducing
discretionary consumption by more than assumed in the
central forecast. Such an effect on spending might be
compounded by a tightening in borrowing constraints

and incentives for housing investors. The scenario
assumes firms respond to weaker demand and margin
pressure with a sharper pullback on (non-data centre)
investment and hiring. Overall, the level of GDP is around
1.0 per cent lower than the central forecasts
by end-2028.
The larger pullback in household and business
spending results in weaker activity and employment
growth, but only modestly lower inflation than in
the central forecast, assuming interest rates and the

exchange rate follow the same path as the central
forecast. The greater caution exhibited by households
pushes the gross household saving ratio up gradually to
a peak of around 17 per cent, where it remains over the
remainder of the forecast period. The greater reduction
Chapter 3  |  OutlookOutlookOutlookOutlookOutlookOutlookOutlook

in hiring pushes the unemployment rate 0.3 percentage
points higher by end-2028, creating more spare capacity
in the domestic economy than in the central forecast
(Graph 3.12). The additional spare capacity in the
economy and the labour market reduces inflationary
pressures somewhat, but the assumption of cost
pass-through by firms limits this effect. Overall, trimmed
mean inflation reaches 21⁄2 per cent only one quarter
earlier than in the central forecast.

Key risk #4 – There remains
considerable uncertainty about the
degree of capacity pressures in the
domestic economy and labour
market.
Despite the recent reassessment, it is possible that

we are still understating the extent of capacity
pressures. While we assess that capacity constraints in
the economy and labour market have been tighter than
previously judged, model-based estimates of the
unemployment rate that is consistent with low and
stable inflation continue to point to greater tightness in
labour market conditions than our central assessment. In
addition, if productivity growth were to remain subdued
over the forecast period, rather than pick up modestly as

assumed, it may suggest that the economy’s supply
capacity would be lower than currently expected. The
implications for inflation would depend on how
households and businesses adjust their spending
and expectations.
Conversely, capacity pressures may be weaker than
expected. The broader suite of full employment
indicators points to somewhat less labour market
tightness than our central assessment. It is also possible
that more of the recent strength in wages growth and

inflation reflects factors other than excess demand, such
as conflict-related cost effects or higher inflation
expectations. In these cases, wages growth and inflation
may moderate more quickly than in the central forecast.
Productivity growth could also recover more quickly
than assumed, including if the anticipated benefits of AI
adoption and other technological developments are
realised sooner.
There is also a risk that the unemployment rate
increases by more than expected if labour supply

remains stronger than assumed. There is a risk that
elevated cost-of-living pressures could support labour
force participation for longer, or to a greater extent, than
assumed. If so, stronger labour supply could result in a
higher unemployment rate than forecast, even if labour
demand evolves broadly as expected.

## Document 347: EcoCharts _ Business creations and bankruptcies - Partial update with monthly data as of September 2026 (2026-11-13)

### Page 1

13/11/2026 1
November 2026
BUSINESS CREATIONS AND BANKRUPTCIES IN FRANCE: DOUBLE-DIGIT GROWTH IN BUSINESS CREATIONS,
LOWER INCREASE IN BANKRUPTCIES, AND STABLE NON-PERFORMING LOANS – PARTIAL UPDATE WITH
MONTHLY DATA AS OF SEPTEMBER 2026
ECO CHARTS | BUSINESS CREATIONS AND BANKRUPTCIES IS A NEW PUBLICATION AIMED AT PROVIDING AN OVERVIEW OF THE DYNAMICS
OF BUSINESS CREATIONS AND BANKRUPTCIES IN FRANCE, AS WELL AS THERISKS ASSOCIATED WITH THE LATTER. WE WILL PUBLISH

REGULAR UPDATES TO THIS PUBLICATION.

### Page 2

Double-digit growth in business creations, a slowdown in bankruptcies and stable non-performing loans
2ECOCHARTSECONOMIC STUDIES
• Business creations rose by 16% y/y as of Q3 2026, marking an acceleration compared with the growth seen in 2025 (5.3% y/y).
• This increase was driven by sectors associated with new information and communication technologies, where the trend is gaining
momentum.
• Meanwhile, business bankruptcies rose by 1.7% y/y as of Q3 2026, according to the data from the Banque de France (vs. 3.6% y/y in

2025). After a more pronounced rise in the first quarter (8% y/y), this increase has since slowed. This moderation could continue, as,
according to Altares, payment delays have decreased to an average of 12.3 days in the second quarter of 2026 (14.1 days a year
earlier), compared with the European average of 13.1 days.
• The rise in business bankruptcies is most pronounced in the services sector, which has simultaneously seen an increase in business

creations. This trend is clear in the retail sector, with a 26% y/y increase in business creations as of Q3 2026, while business
bankruptcies are up 3% y/y.
• In construction and manufacturing, business bankruptcies have been declining since Q2 2026.
• The level of the average ratio of non-performing loans is moderately high and has remained stable for the past year (4.0% of total
loans and advances in Q2 2026). More cyclical than in other sectors, the ratio of non-performing loans in construction reached its

highest level since 2019, at 9.5%.
• The share of underperforming loans, but not yet non-performing (in Stage 2, according to IFRS 9 terminology), which provides an
indication of the future trend in non-performing loans, is stable, standing at 15.4% q/q, and remains below its all-time high (16.1%).
• Despite a significant rise since 2021, the economic weight of bankruptcies — which allows them to be weighted by a proxy for
company size — remains in September, on average, below its Q1 2025 peak (0.54 compared with 0.61, respectively). The economic

weight of bankrupted small businesses decreased but still stands at a high level (2.03).
For more information, please refer to our publication: Chart of the week | French corporates: peaks in creations put peaks in bankruptcies into
perspective. July 22, 2026 – Economic Research – BNP Paribas

## Document 411: NZ Q3 Labour market data - Potential RBNZ implications (2026-11-19)

Source PDF: all report pdf/NZ Q3 Labour market data - Potential RBNZ implications (2026-11-19).pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 11
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

#labour #newzealand
NZ Q3 Labour market data - Potential RBNZ implications
By Kelly Eckhold
Chief Economist, Westpac New Zealand
13:16 November 04 2026 Share
The Q3 labour market statistics were weaker than expected. We discuss potential
implications for the RBNZ noting that a December hike remains more likely than not at this
point.
Q3 Labour market data: potential RBNZ implications
The Q3 labour market data were weaker than RBNZ and our expectations.

### Page 2

Today's labour market data seem unequivocally weaker than expected by either us or the RBNZ. While
likely not a gamechanger for the cycle overall, it will add ammunition to the arguments of the MPC
doves that a pause in interest rate rises will make sense soon - perhaps in December or in the New
Year. It’s likely the case that a bit much was priced into December, so the change in market
probabilities in the wake of today’s data makes sense - although my base case remains that a
December rate rise remains more likely than not.
The message from the RBNZ in the October meeting though was to be careful about pricing in "done
deals" and that the outlook will be data-dependent. As we noted at the time, alongside measures of
inflation expectations, the key domestic data to watch ahead of the RBNZ’s 9 December meeting will
be indicators of what is happening to excess capacity (the output gap) as this will be a key determinant
of the Bank’s assessment of the outlook for inflation over the coming year.
It’s useful to remember in the RBNZ’s forecasting framework that GDP figures are the main
determinant of excess capacity in the economy (i.e., the output gap). The Q2 GDP data told us that the
output gap probably narrowed a bit through Q2, mainly reflecting historical revisions. In light of today’s
labour market numbers, the forecast for growth in Q3 may have some downside risk - but there are
conflicting indicators there, so it’s not that clear. However, it’s probably hard to argue that the level of
excess capacity reduced further in Q3 given today’s data. 
In the eyes of dovish MPC members, the rise in the unemployment rate and other measures important
for the RBNZ's labour market dashboard likely push in the direction of having greater comfort with the
medium-term inflation outlook. The vacancy to unemployment ratio increased a bit, labour constraints
eased a bit, and the unemployment rate has increased. Hence from a labour market perspective it
could be argued that policy could have more time all else equal. The outcome of the election, with the
possibility of a new RBNZ mandate, could increase the prominence of these factors - although this
won’t likely be an explicit factor in the RBNZ’s thinking in December even though it may be in the minds
of individual MPC members.
It could be the case that the argument for increases in the OCR track through 2027 could be
undermined with this data. It’s important to note (probably self-evidently) that there are other data that
will also be relevant. Hence the risk of a pause earlier in the New Year has probably increased with this
data, all else equal. I don't think our forecast peak OCR is really at issue here, but the time taken to get
there could be more flexible. Takes along the lines of "3% is the terminal rate" are very premature and
misplaced, in our view.

A December hike remains more likely than not, but there will be a clear debate to be had between the
MPC hawks and doves.
The forecast peak OCR is likely less impacted than the time to reach that peak. Further pauses in the
tightening cycle could come earlier than currently forecast. 
The outlook for the exchange rate is especially clouded by this data should it take a longer time for
NZ rates to move closer to global norms. 
Watch for updated RBNZ commentary on 10 and 19 November. The Governor’s observation that it
would have been useful to update markets ahead of the October meeting could imply a greater
willingness to comment in the coming weeks.

### Page 3

The outlook for the exchange rate is undermined by this data. I feel very happy about our year-end
target of 79c for the NZD/AUD rate with the risk that a recovery in the NZD takes longer than currently
assumed.
Looking ahead, there are some opportunities for the RBNZ to update markets on their views. Top of
mind is Paul Conway's 10 November speech to the UBS Australasia Conference. The Governor is also
speaking to the Institute of Directors on 19 November. If the RBNZ has something to say, then it might
happen in conjunction with these events (the 10 November speech is titled “How is the NZ economy
tracking”, so it will be difficult for Conway to avoid saying something about how he is interpreting
today’s data). We also note the Governor's comments in the October MPR that it could have been
useful to have been more flexible about updating markets on emerging views of new economic data.
This might increase the likelihood that these events could be used to provide guidance on the RBNZ’s
views of this data (and other emerging events), especially if it feels that the market’s interpretation is at
odds with its own.

Media contact
Kelly Eckhold
Chief Economist

## Document 423: Q3 Wage Price Index_ award wage boost (2026-11-20)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Q3 Wage Price Index_ award wage boost (2026-11-20).pdf
Report source: Westpac IQ
Date: 2026-11-20
Pages: 14
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

#wages #inflation #labour
Q3 Wage Price Index: award wage boost
By Ryan Wells
Economist, Westpac
16:06 November 18 2026 Share
The minimum wage and award decision lifted wages growth, but unevenly – enterprise
arrangements responded more than individual arrangements, consistent with a softer
labour market. Q3 2026: 1.1%qtr, 3.5%yr.
The Wage Price Index (WPI) rose 1.1% in Q3 2026, slightly above Westpac’s forecast and the
market consensus for a 1.0% gain. That increased the annual growth rate to 3.5%yr – stronger than

### Page 2

Wages by Bargaining Stream
the past six months, but a similar pace to 2025.
Today’s data also showcased the impact of the 2026 Fair Work Commission’s Annual Wage Review,
which handed down a larger-than-expected 4.75% increase in award wages. This lifted wages in
aggregate, but the gains were uneven.
A significant share of the increase was driven by health care and social assistance, reflecting pay
rises for state government workers covered by enterprise agreements.

In original terms, the WPI lifted 1.7% in Q3. Awards and enterprise arrangements accounted for 64%
of the lift, while individual arrangements contributed just 36%, the smallest share for Q3 since 2019.
Enterprise arrangements, which are often directly linked to award outcomes, were more responsive,
while individual arrangements were less so compared to previous September quarters. That is
consistent with a softer labour market and recent business survey evidence that suggests the rise in

private sector labour costs following the award decision quickly normalised.
Overall, we see the Q3 result as a step up in the level of wages from the award decision rather than
evidence of building underlying wage pressure but we will be watching whether the strength in
enterprise bargaining persists.

### Page 3

Westpac has long believed focusing on wage inflation by wage bargaining stream can help to
understand how wages respond to the economic cycle. Australia’s labour market is split into three
categories of pay setting methods:
Australian wage adjustments, including the Fair Work Commission’s (FWC) minimum wage and award
rate decisions, tend to occur at the start of the new financial year in July. The FWC handed down a
4.75% increase for 2026, larger than the decisions from 2025 (3.50%) and 2024 (3.75%), but lower

than in 2023 (5.75%).
In original terms, the WPI lifted 1.7% in Q3 2026. Of that increase, 0.50ppts were attributed to awards,
which was broadly in line with our expectations. However, both individual and enterprise arrangements
surprised us to the upside, contributing 0.64ppts and 0.65ppts to the quarterly increase in wages
respectively.
National minimum wage and award rates, regulated by the Fair Work Ombudsman,
Enterprise agreements, or
Individual arrangements.

### Page 4

Compared to the same quarter in previous years, each stream's contribution was the strongest in
absolute terms since 2023, largely because the overall lift was bigger than in 2024 and 2025. After
accounting for the size of the increase, enterprise arrangements were responsible for 64% of the lift,
whereas individual arrangements contributed just 36%, the smallest share since 2019.
While the larger award wage decision pushed up the overall increase, it did not flow through evenly.

Many enterprise agreements are directly linked to award outcomes, either through explicit reference to
award rates or because the award decision sets a benchmark in bargaining, which helps explain why
this stream was more responsive. By contrast, wages set under individual arrangements can be more
discretionary and depend more on employers' circumstances, so they were less responsive than in
previous years. That may reflect a softer labour market, where weaker demand for workers reduces the

pressure on employers to lift pay.
Wages by Sector
By sector, private wages (excl. bonuses) rose 1.0% in Q3, pulling up the annual pace from 3.1%yr to
3.4%yr. This marks the strongest pace of wages growth in the private sector since 2023, at the time of
the Fair Work Commission’s decision to raise award wages by a much larger 5.75%.
Private sector pay is set through a mix of arrangements, including a significant share of individual
arrangements (as discussed above). Today's result suggests this year's award wage decision may

have had some flow-on effect to the broader private sector, though, as noted, the pass-through was
uneven and appears to have been stronger for enterprise arrangements than for individual
arrangements.
There was also an element of both breadth and scale when it comes to private sector wage gains. Half
of all private sector jobs recorded a wage increase in Q3, the largest share since this series began in
2016; and of those jobs that recorded an increase, average hourly wages rose 4.5%, the strongest

since Q3 2023 (5.8%).
Public wages continue to outpace the private sector, this time with a 1.3% lift in Q3, bringing the annual
growth pace to 3.9%yr, the strongest since Q4 2025. As expected, the bulk of the gain was driven by
pay rises under enterprise agreements at the state government level, particularly for front-line workers.
Additionally, given scheduled rises under existing APS-wide enterprise agreements for Commonwealth
employees had already been delivered in previous quarters, the Commonwealth level contribution to

public sector wages growth during Q3 was modest.
Relative to the private sector, the driver of public sector wage gains looks to be more about scale than
breadth – across the 38% of public sector jobs recording a wage increase during the quarter the
average hourly wage change was 4.0%, slightly under the record high of a 4.3% recorded back in Q4
2023.

### Page 5

Summary
The Q3 result shows that the larger minimum wage and award rate decision boosted wages but the
detail suggests the lift was driven more by workers with stronger bargaining power. Awards and
enterprise arrangements, where pay outcomes are more directly tied to the Fair Work Commission’s
decision or to collective bargaining, whereas individual arrangements, which are more exposed to
labour market conditions, contributed the smallest share since 2019.

That is consistent with a softer labour market – the unemployment rate has risen ½ppt since the start of
the year, and we are expecting it to peak at 5.2% mid next year. The NAB business survey points the
same way, with the increase in labour costs reported by private sector firms following the award
decision quickly normalising. This suggests the Q3 result is better read as a temporary step up in the
level of wages from the award wage decision than as evidence of building underlying wage pressure,

though we will be watching whether the strength in enterprise bargaining persists.

《部分電力股評價將更看重監管可預測性》
在2026年美國期中選舉涉及的36場州長選戰中，已
有20州出現暫停資料中心建設或許可的政策訴求。
電價、用水與基礎設施成本的分攤爭議，正改寫各
州爭取AI投資的方向，資金可能從監管混沌的地區，
加速流向規則較為明確的州。德州為這輪監管分化
中最值得關注、也最棘手的地區。德州電網接入申
請佇列已成長到約474吉瓦，其中約九成來自資料

中心，而德州州長已宣布暫停核准新的資料中心接
入州內電網，9月底環境委員會的暫停令進一步擴及
自備電源的離網專案，意味企業即使自己發電，也
未必能繞過許可關卡。
相較之下，在大規模用電需求湧入前，先把監管制
度架好的州或許更具產業前景。懷俄明、明尼蘇達、
愛荷華、威斯康辛、內華達、南卡羅來納與田納西
州為潛在的資金承接者。明尼蘇達已立法防止大客
戶把成本轉嫁給一般用戶，科羅拉多的大負荷費率
方案則要求至少15年合約、80%最低計費需求與提
前退出費，把部分退出風險交還給客戶。電力股的
投資邏輯正在轉向，未來部分公用事業股的評價分
化，將從過去單純看需求規模，轉向更看重獲利品
質與監管可預測性。
《AI資料中心電力需求仍為近期財報重點，但焦點
轉向如何平衡供應瓶頸與監管成本》
隨著3Q26財報陸續披露，美國公用事業公司對AI資
料中心用電需求普遍釋出極為樂觀且迫切的長線成
長展望。以FirstEnergy為例，受惠於毗鄰北弗吉尼
亞數據中心走廊的優勢，西弗吉尼亞州等地的資料
中 心 相 關 負 載 需 求 激 增 137% 達到4.3 吉瓦。

American Electric Power其累積的增量負載承諾已
飆升至56吉瓦，半年內暴增一倍。但公用事業高層
的態度在積極擴產中也帶有審慎風險控管，為了避
免新建發電廠及電網升級的費用侵蝕一般用戶權益，

## Document 502: 【粤开宏观】“供强需弱”的四个层次：找到真问题，分类施策

Source PDF: all report pdf/【粤开宏观】“供强需弱”的四个层次：找到真问题，分类施策.pdf
Report source: 粤开证券
Date: 2026-11-17
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

请务必阅读最后特别声明与免责条款 www.ykzq.com  1 / 7

证券研究报告 | 宏观深度

宏观研究 
 【粤开宏观】“供强需弱”的四个层
次：找到真问题，分类施策 
2026 年 11 月 17 日  投资要点

近期报告  
《【粤开宏观】10 月经济数据回落背后：
短期扰动与内需增长机制变化》
2026-11-16 
《【粤开宏观】10 月出口延续高增：AI 拉
动近四成增长，明年芯片涨价效应或减弱》
2026-11-10 
《【粤开宏观】美股大跌：有哪些原因？对
A 股有何影响？》2026-11-06 
《【粤开宏观】美国中期选举如何影响国内
政治、对华政策及资本市场？》2026-11-04 
《【粤开宏观】中国贸易顺差与外部失衡之
辩：六个关键问题》2026-10-27

导读 
“供强需弱”是对当前中国经济运行特征的一个重要概括，但如果不加区分
地使用，容易将不同性质的经济问题混为一谈，进而影响政策判断。 
事实上，“供强需弱”有四个层次，必须找到真问题，分类施策： 
第一，“供强需弱”主要体现在商品制造，服务领域的问题恰好是反过来的，
即需求很强但供给质量难以满足需求，要大力发展文旅康养家政等服务业、
提振服务消费。 
第二，关系到重大战略领域的“供强”是优势，必须巩固优势。 
第三，房地产、传统基建等部分领域的“供强”则是过剩产能，需要通过反
内卷等方式等让市场出清。 
第四，“供强需弱”的主要矛盾是“需弱”，而且主要是内需弱，外需是很强
劲的。 
至于内需，不要纠结于要投资还是要消费，一个经济体当然是既有投资又有
消费。要通过收入分配、社保制度改革等方式提高消费能力、消费意愿，同
时找到有投资效益的项目扩大投资。

风险提示：外部冲击超预期、宏观政策超预期

### Page 2

宏观深度 
请务必阅读最后特别声明与免责条款 www.ykzq.com  2 / 7

目  录 
一、准确理解供需关系：“供强需弱”并非普遍现象，高质量产品和服务则是“供弱需强” ..................................... 3 
二、“供强需弱”的四个层次：区分矛盾性质，分类施策 ................................................................................................. 3 
（一）战略性领域的“供强”是竞争优势，要坚持巩固和提升 ................................................................................. 3 
（二）房地产、传统基建及光伏等部分领域的“供强”表现为供需失衡，要分类调整 ......................................... 4 
（三）制造业供给较强与服务业优质供给不足并存，要加快补齐服务供给短板 ..................................................... 4 
（四）有效需求不足是当前宏观经济运行的突出矛盾，要把扩大内需放在更加重要的位置 ................................. 5

### Page 3

宏观深度
请务必阅读最后特别声明与免责条款 www.ykzq.com  3 / 7
一、准确理解供需关系：“供强需弱”并非普遍现象，高质量
产品和服务则是“供弱需强”
“供强需弱”是就经济运行的总体情况而言，但严格意义上并不能完整概括中国经
济的供需状况。中国的供给优势主要体现在商品生产特别是工业制造领域。经过长期发
展，中国已经形成体系完整、配套齐全、规模庞大的制造业体系，在中低端产品规模化
生产方面具有显著优势，在部分先进制造领域也形成了较强的国际竞争力。但这并不意
味着所有领域的供给能力都很强。部分高端产品、关键技术环节以及高品质服务领域，
仍然存在明显的供给短板。
尤其在不少服务领域，供需关系恰恰不是“供强需弱”，而是需求潜力较大、优质
供给不足。以文化旅游为例，中国拥有丰富的自然资源和历史文化资源，居民对高品质
旅游、休闲度假和文化体验的需求不断增长，但一些地方的旅游开发仍以景区观光、商
业街区和同质化项目建设为主，千篇一律的人造古镇、仿古街区不少，真正具有文化特
色和体验价值的产品却相对缺乏。

家政服务也存在类似问题。随着双职工家庭增加和人口老龄化加深，育儿、养老、
家庭照护等服务需求不断增长，但由于行业标准不健全、服务质量不稳定、信用体系不
完善，许多家庭难以获得价格合理、专业可靠的服务，不得不更多依靠祖辈照护等家庭
内部方式解决。本来可以通过市场交易实现的部分服务需求，由于供给质量、交易成本
等因素制约，未能充分转化为现实消费。
分析供需关系，不能只看生产能力和消费规模的总量对比，还要看供给能否满足需
求、供需是否适配、潜在需求能否转化为有效需求。供给能力强不等于有效供给充分，
消费规模不足也不等于缺乏消费需求。如果忽视这些差异，把“供强需弱”作为所有行
业的统一判断，就难以真正识别经济运行中的结构性矛盾。
二、“供强需弱”的四个层次：区分矛盾性质，分类施策
进一步看，“供强需弱”需要从四个层次加以分析。前两个层次着重区分“供强”
的不同性质：有的体现为长期形成的产业竞争优势，有的则表现为供给与需求变化之间
的阶段性失衡。第三个层次进一步考察供给结构：制造业总体供给能力较强，但高品质

服务等领域仍然“供弱”，反映出不同产业之间供给能力的不平衡。第四个层次则从供
给侧转向需求侧：改善供给结构虽然有助于释放需求，但不能替代总需求的扩大，有效
需求不足仍是当前宏观经济运行的突出矛盾。四个层次从辨析“供强”的性质，到识别
“供弱”的短板，再到把握“需弱”的宏观约束，逐步深入，也对应着不同的政策着力
点。
（一）战略性领域的“供强”是竞争优势，要坚持巩固和提升
在涉及国家安全、产业安全和国际竞争的战略性领域，较强的供给能力具有超越短
期市场供求的长期价值。粮食、能源、关键基础原材料等领域的供给保障能力，关系经
济安全和应对外部冲击的能力；高端装备、先进制造和部分新兴技术产业的供给能力，
则关系产业链供应链韧性和长期国际竞争力。特别是在国际竞争加剧、全球产业链供应
链加快调整的背景下，完整的产业体系、强大的制造能力和较高的自主可控水平，是中
国经济的重要战略优势。部分产业即使生产能力超过国内市场的即时需求，也可以依托
技术、成本和产业链配套优势参与国际竞争，拓展更广阔的市场空间。因此，不能仅以

国内短期供求关系衡量战略性供给能力的合理性，更不能因短期需求波动而削弱长期形

### Page 4

宏观深度
请务必阅读最后特别声明与免责条款 www.ykzq.com  4 / 7
成的产业竞争优势。
要坚持巩固和提升战略性领域的供给优势，统筹产业安全与经济效率，强化关键核
心技术攻关，增强产业链供应链韧性，推动竞争优势从规模和成本向技术、质量和品牌
转变。同时，要区分战略性供给能力建设与一般性规模扩张，不能因为产业具有战略意
义，就忽视市场需求和投资效率。应充分发挥市场机制作用，强化技术水平、市场前景
和投资效益约束，防止地方政府盲目布局和企业低水平重复投资。
（二）房地产、传统基建及光伏等部分领域的“供强”表现为供需
失衡，要分类调整
与战略性供给优势不同，房地产、部分基础设施以及光伏等新兴产业的部分环节，
面临的是原有供给规模、结构或投资方式与市场需求变化不相适应的问题，但具体成因
有所不同。房地产主要是市场供求关系发生重大变化，随着人口流动、城镇化进程和居
民购房预期变化，一些地区原有开发规模与实际住房需求不再匹配，存量消化和结构调
整压力加大。基础设施建设则是随着发展阶段变化，部分传统投资领域已由补短板阶段

转向相对完善，继续扩大建设规模面临边际收益下降、利用效率不足和债务约束增强等
问题。光伏等新兴产业的部分环节则是在市场前景较好、技术进步较快的背景下，投资
主体集中进入、生产能力快速扩张，短期供给增长快于市场需求增长，导致价格竞争加
剧、企业利润受到挤压。三类问题分别涉及需求结构变化、投资收益变化和产业扩张节
奏变化，虽然都表现为阶段性供需失衡，但调整的重点和方式应有所区别。
房地产应适应供求关系变化，加快存量盘活和住房供给结构优化，更加注重改善居
住品质和满足真实住房需求，推动发展模式从规模扩张转向品质提升。基础设施投资应
更加重视实际需求、项目效益和债务可持续性，减少缺乏需求支撑的低效投资，将更多
资源投向城市更新、公共服务等仍有明显短板的领域。光伏等新兴产业则应完善公平竞
争和市场化退出机制，规范地方招商引资和产业扶持行为，减少同质化投资与“内卷式”
竞争，引导企业从价格竞争转向技术和质量竞争。
（三）制造业供给较强与服务业优质供给不足并存，要加快补齐服
务供给短板
前两个层次主要分析供给能力较强的领域，但从产业结构看，中国经济还存在明显

的供给短板，突出表现为制造业总体实力较强，而部分现代服务业和高品质生活服务业
发展相对滞后。随着居民消费从以商品消费为主逐步向商品与服务消费并重转变，服务
供给的质量、多样性和可获得性对消费增长的影响日益突出。如果服务供给不能适应需
求升级，即使居民具有消费意愿和一定支付能力，也难以形成相应的市场交易。这既制
约了居民生活质量改善，也限制了消费潜力释放和就业机会创造。
服务业供给不足的形成机制与制造业有所不同。制造业可以通过资本投入、技术进
步和规模化生产较快提高供给能力，而养老、托育、家政、文旅等服务业更依赖专业人
才、行业标准、信用体系和制度环境。其中，家政、托育、养老等领域的突出问题是服
务质量难以事先判断、行业规范和信用机制不健全，消费者面临较高的信息成本和信任
风险；文化旅游等领域则更多表现为产品同质化、创新不足，难以满足多样化、品质化
的消费需求。（详见《文旅热潮之下，消费潜力为何未充分释放？——中国文旅消费的特
征、问题与制度成因》）
补齐服务供给短板，既要破除不合理的市场准入限制，鼓励多元化市场主体参与，

扩大优质服务供给，也要完善行业标准、职业培训、信用评价和消费者权益保护机制，

### Page 5

宏观深度
请务必阅读最后特别声明与免责条款 www.ykzq.com  5 / 7
提高服务的专业化、规范化水平。对于托育、养老等兼具公共服务属性的领域，还应通
过适当的财政支持和公共服务供给，提高服务的可及性和可负担性。服务业提质扩容不
仅能够释放尚未充分实现的消费需求，还能够创造就业、增加居民收入，形成供给改善
与需求扩大的良性互动。
（四）有效需求不足是当前宏观经济运行的突出矛盾，要把扩大内
需放在更加重要的位置
前三个层次主要涉及供给能力和供需结构，但供给结构调整不能替代总需求的扩大。
当前，有效需求不足是宏观经济运行的突出矛盾，主要表现为居民消费动力不足、企业
投资意愿偏弱。居民消费不足，既与收入增长和就业预期有关，也受到社会保障不够完
善、教育医疗养老等支出压力较大、预防性储蓄倾向较强等因素影响。企业投资不足，
则主要受市场需求偏弱、盈利预期不稳和投资回报下降等因素制约。消费和投资需求不
足又会影响企业生产经营、就业和居民收入，形成需求不足与预期转弱相互强化的循环。
由此可见，当前经济运行不仅面临供需结构不匹配的问题，还受到宏观总需求不足的制

约，必须在优化供给结构的同时，着力增强内需动力。
扩大内需，关键是增强居民消费能力和意愿，激发企业投资活力。消费方面，要着
力促进就业、增加居民特别是中低收入群体收入，完善收入分配制度和社会保障体系，
减轻教育、医疗、养老等支出负担，降低预防性储蓄倾向，释放消费潜力。投资方面，
要稳定政策和市场预期，改善营商环境，拓展具有真实需求和合理回报的投资空间，增
强民间投资动力，同时优化政府投资结构，提高投资效益。还要加强扩大内需与优化供
给的政策协同，以优质供给满足和创造消费需求，以有效需求引导产业结构优化，促进
消费、投资与生产形成良性循环。
总体来看，“供强需弱”并非简单的供给过强与需求不足，而是不同性质的供需矛盾
相互交织。政策上应坚持分类施策，既要巩固战略性供给优势，推动房地产、传统基建
及相关产业领域的供需再平衡，补齐优质服务供给短板，也要把扩大内需放在更加突出
的位置。只有把供给结构优化与有效需求扩大结合起来，才能推动供需关系在更高水平
上实现动态平衡。

股票投资评级说明 
投资评级分为股票投资评级和行业投资评级。

股票投资评级标准 
报告发布日后的 12 个月内公司股价的涨跌幅度相对同期沪深 300 指数的涨跌幅为基准，投资建议的评级标准为： 
买入：相对大盘涨幅大于 10%； 
增持：相对大盘涨幅在 5%~10%之间； 
持有：相对大盘涨幅在-5%~5%之间； 
减持：相对大盘涨幅小于-5%。

行业投资评级标准 
报告发布日后的 12 个月内行业股票指数的涨跌幅度相对同期沪深 300 指数的涨跌幅为基准，投资建议的评级标准为： 
增持：我们预计未来报告期内，行业整体回报高于基准指数 5%以上； 
中性：我们预计未来报告期内，行业整体回报介于基准指数-5%与 5%之间； 
减持：我们预计未来报告期内，行业整体回报低于基准指数 5%以下。

### Page 1

25 岁 “AI 股神 ”Aschenbrenner 正筹划重启对冲基⾦ Situational Awareness ，计划重引杠杆
并向外部募资，内部称之为 “Situational Awareness 2.0” 。恰逢其持有约 50 亿美元股份的
Anthropic 本周启动 IPO ，为重建计划提供重要⽀撑。
摘要：
今年夏天， Leopold Aschenbrenner 管理的对冲基⾦ Situational Awareness 在短短数天内从管理
逾 200 亿美元资产跌落⾄需要 Ken Griffin 旗下 Citadel 出⼿救援。
如今，这位 25 岁的 "AI 股神 " ⼜⼀次开始筹划重启。
11 ⽉ 18 ⽇，据知情⼈⼠向英国《⾦融时报》透露， Aschenbrenner 近⼏周与多家主经纪商及投
资者讨论了新⼀轮计划，内容包括重新引⼊杠杆交易、向外部投资者募集新资⾦。这⼀重建计
划在相关对话中被称为 "Situational Awareness 2.0" 。恰逢其持有约 50 亿美元股份的 Anthropic

本周启动 IPO ，为重建计划提供重要⽀撑。
虽然叫 "2.0 计划 " ，但这实际上已是 "AI 股神 " 今年的第三场 " 复仇 "—— 今年夏天第⼀次崩盘后的 " 复
仇计划 " 同样以惨败告终，公开持仓从 200 亿美元缩⽔⾄不⾜ 16 亿美元。
第⼀次崩盘：杠杆泡沫的破裂
今年 6 ⽉中旬，市场上出现了⼀个不寻常的信号：总收益互换（ TRS ）的融资成本开始垂直攀
升。这背后，正是 Situational Awareness 在⼤规模借⼊杠杆、追逐 AI 芯⽚⾏情。基⾦资产⼀度⾼
达 450 亿美元，随后在不到⼀个⽉内暴跌⾄ 100 亿美元。公开持仓被以折价批量甩卖给 Citadel ，
所得款项⽤于偿还包括⾼盛和摩根⼤通在内的主经纪商。
事后复盘，整个芯⽚ / 存储器的狂热⾏情，不过是杠杆包装成叙事的泡沫 —— ⽽交易的顶点，恰
好就是 TRS 融资成本创历史新⾼的那⼀天。
这场交易的最⼤赢家，是站在对⾯的⼈。 Citadel 旗舰基⾦在 7 ⽉因接盘 Leopold 的抛售⽽⼤涨

6% ， Griffin 随后数周内⼏乎全部转⼿，坐收数⼗亿美元利润，⼏乎零⻛险。⽽⾼盛主经纪业务
部⻔，据媒体 9 ⽉报道，则从这只基⾦⾝上赚取了逾 2 亿美元费⽤ —— 尽管它们最终也参与了逼
仓。

元， 26 只持仓中 21 只已关闭。剩余仓位中，⾼达 66% 为看涨期权： Bloom Energy 看涨期权（名

义规模扩⼤⼗倍⾄ 3.89 亿美元）、 Nebius 新增看涨期权（ 3.33 亿美元）、 Sandisk 看涨期权（ 1.83
亿美元）、存储 ETF 看涨期权（ 1.4 亿美元），以及 4.41 亿美元的 SharonAI 股票。
但进⼊ 10 ⽉和 11 ⽉， AI ⾏情再次全⾯回调。对冲基⾦连续数⽇抛售 AI 敞⼝，⾼盛主经纪数据显
⽰，对冲基⾦⾃ 2025 年初以来累计买⼊ AI 股票的净头⼨被悉数抹平；全球 AI 股票净配置⽐例从 6
⽉峰值的 31.7% 降⾄ 10 ⽉中旬的 26.1% 。 11 ⽉ 5 ⽇，纳斯达克创下新冠疫情以来最⼤单⽇跌幅，
市场数据显⽰， Leopold 在 6 ⽉持有的那批标的，正是散⼾抛售最集中的品种之⼀。
结果是：⾃ 9 ⽉ 30 ⽇以来，他的公开持仓中每⼀只标的均下跌 36% ⾄ 58% 。那他批 1 ⽉份弹性的
看涨期权，除 AMD 外， Sandisk 需要从当前价格上涨近两倍才能回到⾏权价， CoreWeave 和英
特尔需要涨逾⼀倍，其余标的也需要在两个⽉内上涨 50% ⾄ 80% 。

### Page 4

第三次重启的底⽓： Anthropic IPO
⽀撑这次 "Situational Awareness 2.0" 重建的，是⼀张即将兑现的 " 底牌 " 。
Situational Awareness 持有 Anthropic 约 50 亿美元的股份 —— ⽽ Aschenbrenner 的妻⼦正担任
Anthropic ⾸席执⾏官 Dario Amodei 的幕僚⻓。
本周， Anthropic 正式启动 IPO ，⽬标估值⾼达 1.4 万亿美元。上市完成后，这笔持股的价值预计
将⼤幅提升，为基⾦重建提供重要的资本⽀撑。
据参与相关讨论的⼈⼠透露， Aschenbrenner 还计划在明年年初向外部投资者募集额外资⾦。不
过，杠杆和募资计划尚未最终敲定，仍有变数。
重建的代价：修复信任
重启之路并不平坦。
今年 7 ⽉， Aschenbrenner 曾向投资者公开致歉，宣布基⾦已停⽌向银⾏借款来放⼤押注。如今
重新引⼊杠杆，意味着他必须说服投资者和华尔街银⾏，⾃⼰有能⼒在如此短的时间内重建⻛

险管控体系。
⼀位近期与基⾦管理层交流过的投资者对此态度颇为保留： " 他们所学到的，不过是⻛险管理还
需要再精细调整⼀下。 "
另⼀位投资者则直⾔： " 反复爆仓是很丢⼈的事。所以他真的很想避免再次陷⼊那种局⾯，但我
确实认为他预期⾃⼰会以相当⼤的规模重新管理公开投资组合。 "
在主经纪商关系上， Aschenbrenner 已与 Jefferies 展开谈判，讨论开设主经纪账⼾ —— ⽽
Jefferies 今年早些时候曾以基⾦对冲不⾜为由拒绝了他。此外，基⾦此前已与专业经纪商 Clear
Street 展开合作。
Situational Awareness 和 Jefferies 均拒绝置评。
组织重建：从 8 ⼈到 30 ⼈
在⼈员架构上，这家基⾦正在快速扩张。
今年 6 ⽉的监管⽂件显⽰，基⾦当时仅有 8 名员⼯。据知情⼈⼠透露，⽬前员⼯数量已增⾄约 30
⼈，同时正在招聘⾸席⻛险官 —— 这是⼤型对冲基⾦或⾼杠杆基⾦的核⼼职能之⼀，也是此前
危机中暴露出的最明显短板之⼀。
⽬前，基⾦仍管理着⼀个⼩规模的公开投资组合，根据本周最新发布的监管⽂件，持仓包括

SharonAI 和数据中⼼开发商 CleanSpark 等少数⼏只股票。其余资产超过 100 亿美元，主要为私
募投资。
信徒仍在
尽管经历了这场危机，部分投资者对 Aschenbrenner 依然保持信⼼。
" 所有曾经相信他的⼈，现在可能还是相信他， " ⼀位投资者说。

## Document 564: 価格転嫁の「第二波」に警戒（26年10月企業物価指数） ～川下への転嫁が続くなか、川上価格が再上昇。物価高長期化のリスク～

Source PDF: all report pdf/価格転嫁の「第二波」に警戒（26年10月企業物価指数） ～川下への転嫁が続くなか、川上価格が再上昇。物価高長期化のリスク～.pdf
Report source: DLRI
Date: 2026-11-12
Pages: 4
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Economic Trends

2026 年11 月 12 日

1/4 
価格転嫁の「第二波」に警戒（26 年 10 月企業物価指数） 
～川下への転嫁が続くなか、川上価格が再上昇。物価高長期化のリスク～ 
 経済調査部 シニアエグゼクティブエコノミスト  新家 義貴 
（℡：050-5474-7490）

（要旨） 
○ 10 月の国内企業物価指数は前月比＋0.7％、前年比＋7.1％。前年比では２ヵ月ぶりに＋７％
台へ再加速し、市場予想を大幅に上回る強い結果。農林水産物価格が大幅に下落する一方、
石油・化学関連を中心に幅広い品目で価格が上昇した。 
○ 石油化学関連では、川中の合成樹脂価格が下落し、川下のプラスチック製品では値上がりが
続くという従来の構図に大きな変化はない。一方、川上では資源価格の再上昇を背景に価格
が大きく上昇し、新たなコスト上昇圧力が生じている。 
○ 既往のコスト高の川下への転嫁が続くなか、資源高に伴う川上価格再上昇の影響が今後川中・
川下にも波及する可能性がある。価格転嫁の「第二波」が生じることで、企業物価の上昇圧
力が長期化するリスクが高まっている。 
○ 米価格の大幅下落が続く一方、飲食料品の価格上昇率は高まっており、食料品へのコスト転
嫁圧力も強い。企業段階での物価上昇圧力が消費者物価にも波及することで、27 年初には消
費者物価上昇率が前年比＋３％を上回ると予想する。

1. 事前予想を大幅に上回り、前年比は＋７％台へ再加速 
日本銀行から公表された 2026 年 10 月の国内企業物価指数は、前月比＋0.7％、前年比＋7.1％
となった。前月比では３ヵ月ぶりの上昇であり、伸び率も非常に高い。前年比でも９月の＋6.8％
から伸びを高め、２ヵ月ぶりに＋７％台となっている。市場予想の前年比＋6.5％を大きく上回っ
ており、レンジ（同＋6.1％～＋6.8％）上限も突き抜けた。企業段階での物価上昇圧力が再び強
まっていることが確認される結果である。 
今回注目されるのは、資源価格が上向いたことを背景に、石油・化学関連価格が再び上昇に転
じたことである。９月までは石油・石炭製品や合成樹脂などの価格上昇が一服する一方、プラス
チック製品など川下では値上げが続くという構図となっていた。それに対して 10 月は、川下への
既往のコスト高の価格転嫁が続くなか、川上からの上昇圧力が再び強まった点が特徴である。 
前月比の内訳をみると、石油・石炭製品が同＋2.9％（寄与度：＋0.20％Pt）、化学製品が同＋

### Page 2

2/4 
1.9％（同＋0.15％Pt）と大きく上昇した。このほか、電力・都市ガス・水道が前月比＋2.4％
（寄与度：＋0.15％Pt）、鉄鋼が同＋2.0％（同＋0.11％Pt）、飲食料品が同＋0.6％（同＋0.08％
Pt）、プラスチック製品が同＋1.6％（同＋0.06％Pt）となるなど、幅広い品目で価格上昇がみら
れた。なお、夏季電力料金の影響を除いた国内企業物価指数は前月比＋0.9％であり、この影響を
除けば、10 月の伸びはさらに大きかったことになる。 
一方、農林水産物は前月比▲6.6％（寄与度：▲0.28％Pt）と、９月に続いて大幅に下落した。
前年比でも▲25.0％と大幅なマイナスとなっている。玄米（前月比▲19.0％）、精米（同
▲10.1％）の下落が目立つ。2026 年産米への切り替わりが進むなか、米価の下落が押し下げ要因
となっている。こうした農林水産物の大幅下落にもかかわらず、国内企業物価が前月比＋0.7％
（夏季電力料金調整後：同＋0.9％）と高い伸びになったことは、企業段階での物価上昇圧力の強
さを示している。

2. 川上価格が再び上昇、価格転嫁の「第二波」の可能性も 
石油化学関連では、川上・川中・川下の価格動向に９月までとは異なる動きがみられた。これ
までは、川上・川中の価格にやや落ち着きがみられる一方、川下では春先の原材料高を受けた価
格転嫁が続くという構図だった。10 月も川中の合成樹脂価格は下落し、川下のプラスチック製品
では値上がりが続くなど、川中・川下の動きは概ね変わらない。しかし、川上では資源価格の再
上昇を背景に石油化学関連価格が再び大きく上昇しており、新たなコスト上昇圧力が生じている
点が大きな違いである。 
川上の動きを詳しくみると、石油・石炭製品は前月比＋2.9％と、４ヵ月ぶりに上昇した。この
うちナフサは同＋5.6％となった。また、化学製品に含まれる石油化学系基礎製品も同＋9.1％と
大きく上昇した。内訳では、エチレンが同＋6.6％、プロピレンが同＋6.6％、ベンゼンが同＋
（出所）日本銀行「企業物価指数」

3/4 
7.6％、キシレンが同＋17.1％となるなど、幅広い品目で値上がりがみられた。資源価格の再上昇
が影響したとみられる。 
一方、川中に位置する合成樹脂は前月比▲0.6％と、３ヵ月連続で下落した。ポリエチレンが同
▲1.6％、ポリプロピレンが同▲1.5％となるなど、主要品目でも下落が続いている。川上価格が
再び上昇に転じるなかでも、川中ではこれまでの原材料価格下落の影響が依然残っているとみら
れる。 
これに対して、川下の価格上昇は続いている。プラスチック製品は 10 月に前月比＋1.6％と８
カ月連続で上昇し、前年比でも＋14.1％と９月の＋12.3％から一段と伸びを高めた。品目別で
は、輸送機械用プラスチック製品が前月比＋4.8％、電気機器用プラスチック製品が同＋5.1％と
なったほか、再生プラスチック成形材料も同＋20.7％と大きく上昇した。９月はプラスチック製
容器など包装資材の値上がりが目立ったが、10 月は自動車や電気機器向けの部材でも価格上昇が
進んでいる点が目につく。 
今回重要なのは、既往のコスト高の川下への転嫁が続くなか、新たな価格上昇圧力が川上に生
じていることである。川中・川下ではこれまでの価格動向が概ね続いている一方、川上では資源
価格の再上昇が新たなコスト増につながり始めた。今後、この川上価格の上昇が時間差を伴って
川中・川下へ波及すれば、既往のコスト高の転嫁に新たな値上げが重なることになる。価格転嫁
の「第二波」が生じる可能性があり、物価上昇圧力が長期化することが懸念される。

3. 物価上昇圧力が長期化する可能性 
先行きの企業物価も、高い伸びが続く可能性が高い。10 月の輸入物価指数は円ベースで前月比
＋3.3％と、９月の同▲0.7％から上昇に転じた。契約通貨ベースでも同＋2.3％と上昇している。
なかでも石油・石炭・天然ガスは契約通貨ベースで同＋5.3％、円ベースで同＋6.4％と大きく上
昇した。 
こうした輸入物価上昇は、今後、時間差を伴って国内の川中・川下価格にも波及するとみられ
る。川下では既往のコスト高の価格転嫁がなお続いているほか、物流費や人件費などの上昇も企
業の価格設定に影響している。こうしたなか、新たに資源高によるコスト上昇が加わることで、
物価上昇圧力が長期化する可能性がある。 
こうしたことから、国内企業物価の前年比上昇率は当面＋７％前後の高い伸びが続く可能性が
高い。コメ価格の下落は引き続き下押しとなるものの、資源価格再上昇による新たなコスト増
と、既往のコスト高の価格転嫁が重なることで、物価上昇が落ち着くには時間を要するとみられ
る。 
消費者物価への影響にも注意が必要である。10 月は農林水産物が前年比▲25.0％と大幅に下落
した一方、飲食料品は同＋4.8％と、９月の＋4.4％から伸びを高めた。コメ価格の下落が進むな
かでも、加工食品などではコスト高の価格転嫁が続いていることがうかがわれる。コメ価格の下
落は消費者物価の押し下げ要因となるものの、その他の食料品では値上がりが続くとみられ、食
料品全体では年度後半にかけて価格上昇圧力が強まると予想している。

### Page 4

経済研究所が信ずるに足ると判断した情報に基づき作成していますが、その正確性、完全性に対する責任は負いません。見通しは予告
なく変更されることがあります。また、記載された内容は、第一生命保険ないしはその関連会社の投資方針と常に整合的であるとは限
りません。
4/4
総じてみれば、10 月の企業物価は、これまでのコスト高の価格転嫁が川下で続くなか、資源価
格の再上昇によって川上からの物価上昇圧力も再び強まっていることを示した。今後は新たなコ
スト高が川中・川下へ波及し、価格転嫁の「第二波」が生じる可能性がある。消費者物価につい
ても、こうした企業間取引段階のコスト上昇が時間差を伴って反映されることで、上昇圧力が強
まるとみられる。筆者は、27 年初の消費者物価上昇率が前年比＋３％を上回ると予想している
が、今回の企業物価指数はこうした見通しと整合的な結果だったと考えている。当面、物価の上
振れに警戒が必要な局面が続くだろう。

事件  1
11/18 Fed ⼗⽉會議紀要：幾乎所有官員認為需進⼀步升息
五⼤重點
1  Fed 於美東 11/18 公布 10 ⽉ 27 ⾄ 28 ⽇會議紀要，當時以 10 ⽐ 2 維持利率 3.75% ⾄ 4.00% ，理事庫
克與克⾥夫蘭 Fed 總裁哈瑪克投反對票，主張升息 1 碼。
2   紀要寫明「幾乎所有」與會者認為，若經濟如預期發展，進⼀步升息可能是適當的；有兩位與
會者提出，若通膨數據不利， 12 ⽉可考慮升息超過 1 碼。

### Page 2

3   「絕⼤多數」與會者認為通膨⾵險偏上⾏，原因是能源價格偏⾼與成品油供應受限；多位與會
者指出 AI 建置需求正推升科技產品價格，是重要的通膨⾵險來源。
4   幾乎所有與會者討論 AI 報酬可能不如預期，多數指出 AI 相關債務以複雜且不透明的結構快速增
加；紀要公布後 2 年期公債殖利率升⾄ 4.80% ， S&P 500 收跌 0.24% 。
5  Fed 幕僚預估明年通膨隨能源回落⽽明顯下降、 2028 年回到約 2% ，失業率到 2029 年都低於⻑
期估計值；紀要稱就業市場穩定、接近充分就業。
三⼤結論
1   紀要確認 10 ⽉暫停是時機選擇⽽⾮政策轉向，與美國 10 ⽉核⼼ CPI ⽉增 0.30% 、⾮農新增 16.5
萬⼀致， 12 ⽉升息 1 碼仍是基準；⻑端殖利率維持 5% 以上，⻑久期股票與⻑債的折現壓⼒未解
除。
2  Fed 把 AI 相關債務列為⾦融穩定議題，與⾼槓桿新雲端以外部融資擴產的⾵險相互印證；評價
仍應區分有⾃由現⾦流的上游與依賴再融資的中游，不宜把 AI 供應鏈視為同⼀部位。

3   當 12 ⽉前公布的核⼼服務價格與房租再加速、市場開始定價 2 碼時，應維持短天期國庫券為利
率⾵險核⼼並延後增加⻑債；若核⼼⽉增回落且標售需求改善，再逐步延⻑存續期間。
事件  2
11/18 Fed 理事華勒主張 12 ⽉升息 2 碼，巴爾與戴利也⽀持續升息
五⼤重點
1  Fed 理事華勒 11/18 接受 CNBC 訪問表⽰，若 Fed 不明確釋出 1 ⽉再升息的訊號， 12 ⽉就應升息 2
碼；他認為只說「保留可能」不會影響市場定價。
2   華勒說明，通膨已連續近六年⾼於 2% ⽬標且正在擴散， Fed 需要收回去年的三次降息；紐約
Fed 總裁威廉斯前⼀⽇（ 11/17 ）則表⽰情勢不需要劇烈⾏動。
3  Fed 理事巴爾同⽇演說指出，通膨過⾼、正在壓迫許多家庭，⽽經濟強勁、失業率低、消費穩
健，若經濟如預期發展，他可能⽀持進⼀步緊縮。
4   舊⾦⼭ Fed 總裁戴利同⽇表⽰仍需升息使通膨回到 2% ，但應謹慎、漸進；她指出所得分布下半
部家庭的消費仍有韌性，這既是優勢也是⾵險。

5   市場仍未接受 2 碼情境：利率預測市場對 12 ⽉⼤幅升息的機率僅約 9% ；美元指數升⾄約
103.8 ，為 2025 年 4 ⽉以來⾼點。

### Page 3

三⼤結論
1  Fed 內部分歧已從「是否升息」轉為「升息幅度與節奏」，華勒的 2 碼主張與紀要中兩位與會者
的看法⼀致， 12 ⽉會議前的通膨數據將決定是 1 碼加前瞻指引，還是⼀次 2 碼。
2   鷹派發⾔推升美元並壓抑貴⾦屬，印證短線美元流動性與短券的相對優勢；但官員對節奏仍有
歧⾒，不宜把單⼀官員主張直接視為 Fed 決議。
3   當 12 ⽉會議前的核⼼ CPI 與房租外服務續強、利率期貨定價 2 碼機率明顯升⾼時，應降低⻑久
期與⾼估值成⻑股權重、保留短券；若核⼼降溫使官員回到漸進路徑，再分批回補品質股。
事件  3
11/18 Anthropic IPO 定價每股 83 美元，⾼於原區間，估值約 1.5 兆美元
五⼤重點
1  Anthropic 於美東 11/18 確定 IPO 價格為每股 83 美元，⾼於原定 70 ⾄ 78 美元區間，完全稀釋後估
值約 1.5 兆美元， 11/19 起在那斯達克以代號 ANT 交易。
2   公司原計畫出售約 8.25 億股，因需求強勁擴⼤發⾏規模但未公布最終股數；以原股數計算募資

約 685 億美元（推算），另有科技企業認購 260 億美元無表決權股份。
3   估值⾼於 5 ⽉私募輪的 9,650 億美元，但低於數⽉前傳出的 2 兆美元⽬標；知情⼈⼠指出機構認
購意向約 4,000 億美元、散戶約 1,000 億美元。
4   定價前⼀⽇科技股連續震盪， Cboe 預計最快在掛牌後第⼆個交易⽇推出選擇權；七位共同創
辦⼈各出售近 340 萬股。
5   招股⽂件顯⽰今年上半年營收約為去年同期 13 倍、第⼆季⾸度單季營業獲利；但 Token 降價與
企業控管 AI 預算，仍是收⼊成⻑的主要變數。
三⼤結論
1   定價⾼於區間代表資本市場仍願意為具收⼊成⻑的模型企業提供⼤規模資⾦， AI 資本⽀出的融
資管道暫未收縮；但 1.5 兆美元估值已先反映⾼成⻑，上市後股價對季度收⼊更敏感。
2   模型企業的價值取決於⽤量成⻑能否⼤於 Token 降價、企業預算是否擴張，以及新商業模式能
否放量；年化收⼊與已實現收⼊、總額與淨額認列需分開檢視，不宜以 IPO 熱度推論所有 AI 軟體

受惠。

### Page 4

3   當 Anthropic 上市後⾸份季報顯⽰付費⽤量與⽑利同步改善時，應維持有現⾦流的上游與優質
軟體配置；若收⼊成⻑放緩且股價跌破發⾏價，應降低⾼估值 AI 軟體與⾼槓桿雲端曝險。
事件  4
11/18 現貨⾦價跌⾄ 2025 年 9 ⽉來低點，倫敦銅創 4 ⽉來新低
五⼤重點
1   現貨⿈⾦ 11/18 跌約 3.27% ⾄每盎司約 3,722.6 美元，為 2025 年 9 ⽉以來最低； COMEX 12 ⽉期
⾦結算 3,741.9 美元，現貨⽩銀跌 4.95% ⾄ 44.93 美元。
2   ⾦屬賣壓主因是美元與升息預期：美元指數升⾄約 103.8 ，為 2025 年 4 ⽉以來⾼點， Fed 紀要與
官員鷹派發⾔強化進⼀步升息的預期，美伊緊張也未引發避險買盤。
3   倫敦⾦屬交易所三個⽉期銅盤中跌逾 6% ⾄每噸 12,430 美元，收跌 756 美元⾄ 12,471 美元；
COMEX 期銅同步創 7 個⽉低點，為連續第五個交易⽇下跌。
4   銅價下跌來⾃投機部位撤出與程式交易，價格跌破技術⽀撐後賣壓擴⼤；資⾦成本上升壓抑⾵

險偏好， AI 資料中⼼需求題材在科技股⽌穩下也未能撐住銅價。
5   上海期貨交易所夜盤同步重挫：滬⾦收跌 2.30% 、滬銀收跌 4.23% 、國際銅收跌 4.59% ；⿈⾦
在 11/6 ⾄ 13 ⽇已下跌 4.60% ，本次為再⼀次加速下跌。
三⼤結論
1   ⿈⾦短線受美元與實質利率同步⾛升壓制，事件期未發揮避險功能，與先前下修⿈⾦短期評分
⽅向⼀致；⿈⾦仍保留分散財政與貨幣信⽤尾端的功能，但不宜作為唯⼀避險。
2   銅價跌破關鍵價位反映中國傳統需求不⾜與資⾦⾯收緊，電網與 AI 需求雖在，但無法抵銷投機
部位撤出；銅宜分批配置，避免把 AI 題材視為價格⽀撐的保證。
3   當核⼼通膨回落、美元與實質利率停⽌同步上升時，應逐步回補⿈⾦分散部位；若庫存累積⽽
電網與 AI 訂單放慢，應下修銅的配置，⽩銀維持⼩型衛星不追反彈。
事件  5
11/18 ECB 副總裁武伊契奇稱 12 ⽉升息可能性⾮常⼤
五⼤重點

### Page 5

1   歐洲央⾏副總裁武伊契奇 11/18 表⽰，依⽬前情況 12 ⽉升息的可能性⾮常⼤，薪資與物價的⼆
次傳導效應在 2027 年「肯定」會出現； ECB 存款機制利率⽬前為 2.50% 。
2   同⽇歐盟統計局公布 10 ⽉ HICP （調和消費者物價指數）終值年增 4.0% ，與初值相同，創 2023
年以來新⾼；能源價格年增約 21% ，是主要推升⼒量。
3  10 ⽉核⼼ HICP 終值年增 2.6% ，⾼於公布前預期的 2.5% （預期以初值為基礎形成）； HICP ⽉增
0.5% ，也⾼於預期 0.4% ，顯⽰能源以外的價格壓⼒略升。
4   武伊契奇同⽇發布⾦融穩定評估，指出 AI 估值建⽴在⾼獲利預期上， AI 基礎建設愈來愈依賴舉
債，市場情緒轉向時虧損可能由股市傳導⾄債市。
5   歐元兌美元 11/18 跌⾄約 1.0965 ，創 19 個⽉新低；知情⼈⼠稱 ECB 將於 11/25 討論把銀⾏最低準
備⾦率由 1% 提⾼⾄ 2% ，決定可能延⾄ 12 ⽉。
三⼤結論

1   核⼼ HICP ⾼於預期與副總裁的明確表態，使 12 ⽉升息由研究基準變得更接近政策共識；但能
源仍是主要推升因素，薪資成⻑已降⾄ 3.0% ，⼆次傳導是 2027 年的⾵險⽽⾮已發⽣的事實。
2   升息與能源成本同時壓抑企業回報，法國財政⾵險仍在，歐股維持中性偏審慎，偏好低負債、
具海外收⼊與定價能⼒者；歐元的利差⽀撐被能源進⼝與成⻑落差抵銷，維持中性偏低配置。
3   當 12 ⽉確定升息且核⼼ HICP 續升、薪資重新加速時，應降低歐洲週期股與歐元曝險；若能源
回落使總體通膨下降、 PMI 廣度改善，再考慮增加歐洲週期性部位。
事件  6
11/18 美國原油庫存意外增加 338 萬桶， WTI 結算 82.31 美元
五⼤重點
1  EIA （美國能源資訊署） 11/18 公布上週原油庫存增加 338 萬桶，市場預估僅增加約 5.8 萬桶；汽
油庫存增加 519 萬桶，餾分油增加 157 萬桶，增幅皆⾼於預期。
2  EIA 資料同時顯⽰美國週度原油產量連續第四週創新⾼，東岸煉油廠開⼯率升⾄ 2023 年 1 ⽉以

來最⾼，供給⾯改善使油價承壓。
3  WTI 12 ⽉期貨盤中跌逾 4% ⾄約 81.3 美元，結算 82.31 美元；布蘭特收在約 89.17 美元， 11/17 收
盤為 93.14 美元，單⽇跌幅約 4.3% （推算，合約⽉份不同）。

### Page 6

4   美國國防部⻑赫格塞思同⽇突訪卡拉卡斯，與委內瑞拉簽署軍事合作協議；他稱雙⽅⽯油協議
使荷莫茲海峽成為「⾮關鍵因素」，但未公布供油量等細節。
5   跌幅受荷莫茲海峽航運攻擊限制： 11/17 有船隻在海峽遭襲擊、 1 名船員失蹤；美國國家情報總
監同⽇表⽰來⾃伊朗的威脅已⼤幅升⾼。
三⼤結論
1   美國庫存與產量雙增顯⽰實體供給正在修復，油價已遠離 10 ⽉布蘭特 116 美元⾼點；能源通膨
回落有助緩和整體物價，但對核⼼服務通膨的影響需數⽉才會反映。
2   美委協議缺乏供油量細節，荷莫茲航運攻擊仍在，地緣溢價可能反覆；原油宜維持⼩型事件部
位，能源股保留低成本、低負債⽣產商，不宜以單⽇跌幅追空。
3   當美伊和談維持、美國庫存連續累積且裂解價差回落時，應降低能源類股戰術權重；若荷莫茲
航運中斷再擴⼤、庫存轉降，才重新提⾼原油避險部位。
事件  7
11/18 騰訊第三季營收年增 8% ，⾃由現⾦流負 691 億元
五⼤重點
1   騰訊 11/18 港股收盤後公布第三季營收 2,073.4 億元⼈⺠幣，年增 8% ，低於市場預估 2,105.3 億

元；營業利益 591.5 億元，年減 7% ，低於預估 665.9 億元。
2   第三季資本⽀出付款 651 億元，年增 225% ；⾃由現⾦流為負 691 億元，公司說明主因是預付算
⼒採購款，排除後⾃由現⾦流為正 169 億元。
3   排除新 AI 產品的營業利益為 833 億元、年增 8% ，利潤率維持 40% ；公司歸屬淨利 636 億元，年
增 0.8% ，⾮國際會計準則淨利 665 億元，年減 6% 。
4   雲端收⼊年增 35% ，管理層歸因於 WorkBuddy 、模型即服務與 GPU 租賃需求， WorkBuddy ⽇
活躍⽤戶逾 1,000 萬；⾏銷服務收⼊年增 19% ⾄ 430 億元。
5   傳統業務放緩：國際遊戲收⼊年減 9% ⾄ 189 億元，社交網路收⼊年減 1% ⾄ 318 億元；財報公布
後主要股東 Prosus 股價下跌 5.9% 。
三⼤結論
1   騰訊與美國雲端巨頭同樣出現「 AI 收⼊加速、⾃由現⾦流轉負」，顯⽰ AI 投資與營收認列的時

間差已擴及中國平台；評價重點應放在雲端收⼊能否覆蓋算⼒預付款與折舊。

### Page 7

2   營收低於預期且社交與國際遊戲轉弱，呼應中國 10 ⽉零售僅年增 0.4% 的內需疲弱；中國股市
宜維持選擇性衛星部位，內需平台不宜全⾯提⾼權重。
3   當騰訊後續季度的算⼒預付款轉為雲端收⼊、⾃由現⾦流回正，且中國零售與⺠間投資連續改
善時，才考慮提⾼中國網路平台權重；若資本⽀出續增⽽營收維持個位數成⻑，應維持低配。
事件  8
11/18 習近平深圳迎接川普，中美俄三⽅會晤預計 11/19 舉⾏
五⼤重點
1   中國國家主席習近平 11/18 下午在深圳寶安機場親⾃迎接出席 APEC 領導⼈⾮正式會議的美國總
統川普，以回應 9 ⽉訪美時川普夫婦的機場迎接禮遇。
2   習近平表⽰期待就中美關係、亞太合作與國際情勢深⼊交流，建⽴以尊重、公平、互惠為基礎
的建設性戰略穩定關係；川普稱此⾏將推進雙邊關係並取得圓滿成功。
3   克⾥姆林宮 11/14 宣布，普丁、習近平與川普將於 11/19 在深圳舉⾏三⽅會晤，議程尚未公布；
俄⽅稱普丁與川普 11/18 未進⾏實質會談。
4   中國國台辦 11/18 表⽰台灣問題純屬中國內政，三⽅會晤資訊將適時發布；美國眾議院另⼀委

員會同⽇致函輝達暫代執⾏⻑，要求說明晶⽚出⼝情況。
5   會晤前背景：美國商務部 11/16 已將受限制實體使⽤遠端雲端算⼒納⼊出⼝管制；三⽅會晤結
果與任何晶⽚、關稅協議截⾄發稿均尚未公布。
三⼤結論
1   ⾼規格接待顯⽰中美雙⽅有意穩定關係，但截⾄⽬前沒有公布任何具體協議，出⼝管制規則仍
有效；在正式⽂件公布前，不宜預先推算對 AI 晶⽚海外收⼊的影響。
2   ⼈⺠幣與中國股市應分開判讀：境內美元供給偏多⽀撐⼈⺠幣，但內需仍弱，⼈⾏已提⾼外匯
存款準備⾦率抑制單向升值；峰會氣氛本⾝不⾜以構成全⾯提⾼中國資產權重的理由。
3   當會晤後公布的正式⽂件涉及晶⽚出⼝許可或關稅調整時，應逐項核對適⽤範圍與⽣效⽇，再
調整半導體與中國出⼝技術股權重；若僅有原則性聲明，維持既有配置。
事件  9
11/18 ⽇本 10 ⽉出⼝年增 20.5% ，創單⽉出⼝⾦額新⾼

### Page 8

五⼤重點
1   ⽇本財務省 11/18 公布 10 ⽉出⼝年增 20.5% ，略⾼於市場預估 20% ，出⼝⾦額約 11.8 兆⽇圓，創
單⽉歷史新⾼，已連續 14 個⽉成⻑； 9 ⽉為年增 16.5% 。
2  10 ⽉進⼝年增 21.6% ，低於預估 25.8% ，⾦額約 12.2 兆⽇圓，同樣創單⽉新⾼，但進⼝數量年
減 0.2% ；貿易逆差 4,765 億⽇圓，連續第 6 個⽉逆差，⼩於預估的 9,560 億⽇圓。
3   出⼝成⻑由 AI 投資帶動的半導體與晶⽚製造設備出貨⽀撐；對美國出⼝年增 23.9% ，對中國年
增 13.3% ，對歐盟年增 22.1% 。
4   進⼝⾦額上升主要反映原油價格與運費偏⾼，中東航運受阻使⽇本 10 ⽉⾃美國進⼝⽯油⾦額⼤
增；能源成本是貿易持續逆差的主因。
5   背景：⽇本 11/16 公布第三季 GDP 意外萎縮；⽇銀總裁植⽥和男預定 11/19 上午出席國會，市場
關注其對 12 ⽉升息的說法。
三⼤結論
1   ⽇本外需在 AI 設備與半導體帶動下持續強勁，與第三季 GDP 萎縮、家庭實質消費疲弱形成

「外熱內冷」；企業盈利仍有⽀撐，⽇股宜偏重設備、材料與具現⾦流的企業。
2   能源進⼝推升逆差，弱⽇圓提⾼進⼝成本、強⽇圓則壓縮出⼝收⼊，匯率不能單向視為利多；
⽇銀下次升息研究情境仍為 12 ⽉或明年 1 ⽉，但內需疲弱限制升息速度。
3   當⽇本實質消費改善、⽇銀明確釋出 12 ⽉升息訊號時，可分批提⾼⽇股與⽇圓部位；若內需
持續萎縮使升息延後，應維持⽇股選股⽽⾮指數全⾯加碼，⽇本⻑債續維持低配。
事件  10
11/18 外資賣超台股 765.6 億元，三⽇累計賣超 1,848 億元
五⼤重點
1   台股加權指數 11/18 收 40,194.91 點，上漲 102.80 點、漲幅 0.26% ，成交⾦額約 1.03 兆元；櫃買
指數下跌 0.31% 。
2   外資及陸資當⽇賣超 765.64 億元，三⽇累計賣超 1,848.47 億元；投信買超 175.58 億元，⾃營商
賣超 41.92 億元，三⼤法⼈合計賣超 632.07 億元。
3   台積電收 2,175 元、上漲 5 元，⼤盤在外資⼤幅提款下仍維持⼩漲，主要由投信與內資承接；新

台幣兌美元盤中貶⾄約 32.98 。

### Page 9

4   前⼀⽇（ 11/17 ）台股下跌 947 點收 40,092 點；外資賣超延續 11/16 單⽇重挫 6.03% 以來的去槓
桿賣壓。
5   ⾼盛 11/17 將台股評等由「加碼」降為「中⽴」，加權指數⽬標由 57,500 點下調⾄ 50,000 點；台
灣 10 ⽉外銷訂單預定 11/20 公布，研究預估 1,125 ⾄ 1,159 億美元。
三⼤結論
1   外資連續⼤額賣超反映 AI 評價與資⾦⾯的重新定價，但台灣 10 ⽉出⼝年增 58.9% 、台積電 10 ⽉
營收年增 59.7% ，顯⽰基本⾯交付仍在；槓桿調整不等於訂單崩潰，也不保證估值已⾒底。
2   外資匯出與美國升息預期同時壓抑新台幣，股市基本⾯與匯率應分開管理；台股以現⾦流較強
的晶圓代⼯為核⼼， ODM 與⾼估值中游需檢查客戶融資、應收與⽑利。
3   當 11/20 外銷訂單符合或⾼於預估、外資賣超收斂且融資維持率回穩時，可分批增持晶圓代⼯
與先進封裝；若訂單低於預估且回款轉弱，應降低 ODM 與⾼槓桿部位，並提⾼新台幣以外的流

動性。

## Document 694: 报道：欧洲央行拟讨论将最低准备金率由1%上调至2%

Source PDF: all report pdf/报道：欧洲央行拟讨论将最低准备金率由1%上调至2%.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-18
Pages: 2
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

据媒体报道，欧洲央⾏管理委员会计划于 11 ⽉ 25 ⽇召开的会议上讨论，拟将最低准备⾦率
由现⾏ 1% 上调⾄ 2% 。该举措每年可为欧洲央⾏及 21 家欧元区国家央⾏节省⾄少 44 亿欧
元，同时回收部分系统内过剩流动性。
摘要：
欧洲央⾏（ ECB ）正考虑提⾼商业银⾏的最低准备⾦率，此举将直接削减央⾏体系的利息⽀
出，同时回收部分系统内过剩流动性。
据路透援引四位知情⼈⼠透露，欧洲央⾏管理委员会计划于 11 ⽉ 25 ⽇召开的会议上讨论这⼀⽅
案，拟将最低准备⾦率由现⾏ 1% 上调⾄ 2% 。
按当前利率⽔平测算，该举措每年可为欧洲央⾏及 21 家欧元区国家央⾏节省⾄少 44 亿欧元（约
48 亿美元）。知情⼈⼠表⽰，最终决定或将推迟⾄ 12 ⽉ 17 ⽇的货币政策会议上作出。
此次讨论的背景是，欧洲央⾏持续加息导致央⾏体系利息⽀出⼤幅攀升，德国联邦银⾏等机构
因早年⼤规模购债、持有⼤量低收益乃⾄负收益债券，如今⾯临⽇益沉重的财务压⼒。上调准
备⾦率将为央⾏减亏提供财务依据，亦具备收紧货币政策的理论⽀撑。

节省空间可观，利率⾛⾼将进⼀步扩⼤收益
商业银⾏⽬前需将存款及其他短期负债的 1% 存⼊央⾏的⽆息账⼾，超出部分则按欧洲央⾏存款
利率计息。欧元体系⽬前每年需为逾 2 万亿欧元的超额流动性⽀付约 522 亿欧元利息。
将最低准备⾦率提⾼⾄ 2% ，意味着更⼤⽐例的银⾏资⾦将不再计息，从⽽直接压缩利息⽀出。
据路透社测算，按现⾏利率⽔平，此举每年可节省⾄少 44 亿欧元。
随着欧洲央⾏存款利率预计从⽬前的 2.5% 进⼀步升⾄明年的 3% ⾄ 3.25% 区间，上述节省规模还
将相应扩⼤。
过剩流动性是问题根源
欧元区银⾏体系当前超额流动性规模庞⼤，根源在于欧洲央⾏ 2015 年⾄ 2022 年间实施的⼤规模
资产购买计划。彼时，欧洲央⾏通过⼤量购债向银⾏系统注⼊资⾦，旨在遏制通缩、刺激经济
增⻓。
然⽽，⾼评级国家的国家央⾏当年购⼊的⼤量债券收益率极低甚⾄为负。随着政策利率快速上
升，这些央⾏的融资成本⼤幅增加，导致部分机构承受显著亏损。
上调准备⾦率还具备货币政策层⾯的意义 —— 有助于吸收多年宽松政策遗留的过剩流动性，为

政策制定者提供了财务与货币政策的双重理由。
值得注意的是，欧洲央⾏此前曾于 2012 年欧债危机期间将最低准备⾦率由 2% 下调⾄ 1% 。准备
⾦率曾被视为重要的流动性缓冲⼯具，但危机后推出的流动性覆盖率等监管要求已在很⼤程度
上取代了其原有功能。
多数⾏⻓⽀持，内部阻⼒已减弱
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

## Document 695: 拼多多大涨！Q3营收1083亿元不及预期，经营利润同比增长25%｜财报见闻

Source PDF: all report pdf/拼多多大涨！Q3营收1083亿元不及预期，经营利润同比增长25%｜财报见闻.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-19
Pages: 4
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

拼多多第三季度营收同⽐基本持平，但调整后每 ADS 收益超预期。在线营销收⼊增⻓
12% ，交易服务收⼊下降 12% ，业务表现分化。销售与营销费⽤同⽐下降 17% ，推动经营
利润增⻓ 25% ⾄ 312 亿元，但受投资收益减少及所得税费⽤增加影响，归⺟净利润仅增⻓
0.7% 。拼多多周四收涨 11% 。
摘要：
拼多多第三季度营收基本持平且低于市场预期，但调整后每 ADS 收益超预期。尽管收⼊增⻓放
缓，费⽤⽀出收缩仍推动公司经营利润实现两位数增⻓，但净利润基本持平。
11 ⽉ 19 ⽇，拼多多公布截⾄ 2026 年 9 ⽉ 30 ⽇的第三季度未经审计财务业绩。公司当季实现营收
1083.41 亿元⼈⺠币，同⽐增⻓约 0.1% ，低于市场预估的 1152.1 亿元； Non-GAAP 摊薄每 ADS
收益为 21.04 元，⾼于市场预估的 18.54 元。
业务⽅⾯，公司两⼤收⼊板块表现分化。在线营销服务及其他收⼊同⽐增⻓ 12% ，⾄ 598.19 亿
元；交易服务收⼊同⽐下降 12% ，⾄ 485.22 亿元，后者下滑抵消了在线营销业务的部分增⻓。

盈利⽅⾯，销售与营销费⽤同⽐下降 16.6% ，带动总运营费⽤下降 12.3% ，推动经营利润增⻓
24.5% ⾄ 311.64 亿元。不过，受利息及投资净收⼊减少、所得税费⽤增加等因素影响，公司归⺟
净利润仅增⻓ 0.7% ⾄ 295.41 亿元， Non-GAAP 净利润则同⽐下降约 1.2% 。
管理层表⽰，公司仍处于战略投资阶段，未来将继续加强供应链建设和商家⽣态⽀持。财报公
布后，拼多多周四收涨 11% 。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

营收结构分化：在线营销增⻓，交易服务收⼊下降
第三季度，拼多多两⼤业务板块表现分化，在线营销服务收⼊增⻓，但交易服务收⼊出现下
滑。
在线营销服务及其他收⼊为 598 亿元，同⽐增⻓ 12% ，继续保持增⻓。该业务增⻓反映出平台⼴
告变现能⼒仍在提升，商家营销需求为收⼊提供了⽀撑。
交易服务收⼊为 485 亿元，同⽐下降 12% 。管理层在财报声明中提及，全球业务正在适应不断变
化的贸易和监管环境。受此影响，跨境业务⾯临的外部环境仍存在不确定性。
两项业务收⼊⼀增⼀降，抵消了部分增⻓，使公司整体营收维持在去年同期⽔平。后续交易服
务收⼊能否企稳，将是影响公司营收表现的重要因素。
费⽤⽀出下降：销售营销收缩，研发投⼊增加
第三季度，拼多多总运营费⽤为 319 亿元，同⽐下降 12% ，费⽤结构呈现销售营销⽀出减少、研
发投⼊增加的特点。

### Page 3

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。
其中，销售与营销费⽤为 253 亿元，较去年同期的 303 亿元减少 50 亿元，同⽐下降约 17% ，是运
营费⽤下降的主要来源。这⼀变化与公司调整市场推⼴投⼊、优化营销⽀出有关。
与之相对，研发费⽤由去年同期的 43 亿元增⾄ 51 亿元，同⽐增⻓约 17% ，显⽰公司仍在加⼤技
术研发投⼊。⼀般及⾏政费⽤为 16 亿元，低于去年同期的 18 亿元，整体变动不⼤。
在收⼊基本持平的情况下，运营费⽤下降改善了公司的盈利表现。不过，销售营销⽀出的减少
能否在不影响⽤⼾活跃度和商家经营的情况下持续转化为经营效率提升，仍有待后续观察。
经营利润增⻓ 25% ，⾮经营性收⼊与税费影响净利润
第三季度，拼多多营收成本为 452 亿元，较去年同期的 468 亿元下降约 3% 。在营收保持稳定的情
况下，成本⽀出减少，推动⽑利空间有所改善。

成本下降与运营费⽤收缩共同⽀撑了经营利润增⻓。公司当季经营利润达到 312 亿元，较去年同
期的 250 亿元增⻓约 25% ； Non-GAAP 经营利润为 326 亿元，同⽐也实现增⻓。
不过，经营利润的改善并未完全传导⾄净利润。第三季度，公司利息及投资净收⼊为 54 亿元，
低于去年同期的 86 亿元，同⽐下降约 37% ；所得税费⽤则由 40 亿元升⾄ 66 亿元，增⻓约 65% 。
受上述因素影响，公司归属于普通股股东的净利润为 295 亿元，仅较去年同期的 293 亿元⼩幅增
⻓。剔除股权激励费⽤后的 Non-GAAP 净利润为 310 亿元，低于去年同期的 314 亿元。经营利润
与净利润表现出现分化，反映出⾮经营性收⼊减少及税费增加对最终盈利的影响。
现⾦储备保持充裕，管理层继续聚焦供应链投资
第三季度，拼多多经营活动产⽣的净现⾦为 430 亿元，低于去年同期的 457 亿元。前三季度累计
经营活动净现⾦流达到 851 亿元，同⽐增⻓约 3% ，整体现⾦创造能⼒保持稳定。

投资活动⽅⾯，当季净现⾦流出 378 亿元，明显⾼于去年同期的 116 亿元。与此同时，公司固定
资产由年初的 13 亿元增⾄ 64 亿元，⻓期债券投资也有所增加，反映出资⾦配置和投资活动有所
加⼤。
截⾄ 9 ⽉ 30 ⽇，公司现⾦、现⾦等价物及短期投资合计约 4554 亿元；总资产达到 6880 亿元，总
负债为 2123 亿元，股东权益约 4757 亿元，资产负债结构整体稳健。此外，其他⾮流动资产由年
初的 1047 亿元增⾄ 1157 亿元，其中包括定期存款及各类债券投资。
管理层在财报中进⼀步明确了公司的⻓期投⼊⽅向。联席董事⻓兼联席 CEO 赵佳臻表⽰，公司
将继续深化对商家⽣态的⽀持，帮助商家开发优质产品、触达更多消费者。
整体来看，拼多多第三季度呈现出营收增⻓放缓、经营利润改善的特点。在线营销业务保持增
⻓，但交易服务收⼊下滑对整体营收形成制约；销售营销费⽤下降则为经营利润提供了⽀撑。
随着公司继续加⼤供应链和研发投⼊，未来如何平衡业务增⻓、投⼊规模与盈利能⼒，将成为
市场关注的重点。

(株)日本総合研究所 欧州経済展望 2026年10月
◆財部門が生産活動の重石に
足元のユーロ圏景気は、サービス主導で底堅く推
移。７月のサービス業生産は増勢を維持。一方、
鉱工業や建設業など財部門の生産活動は低調。
先行きも、生産活動の二極化は続く見通し。旺盛
なＡＩ需要を背景に、情報通信を中心にサービス
業は好調を維持するものの、エネルギーコストの高止
まりや対中競争力の低下を受けて、中間財や消費
財の生産は低迷が長引く公算大。加えて、金利高
による需要減少が、建設活動を抑制する見込み。
◆物価や金利の上昇が内需を下押し
先行きの景気は、インフレ加速や金利上昇を受け
て減速する見通し。まず、物価高が個人消費を下
押しする公算。９月の消費者物価は前年比＋
3.8％と、前月から伸びが拡大。資源高が続くことで、
来年初にかけてインフレ率がさらに上昇すると予想。
加えて、金利上昇が住宅投資や設備投資を抑制
する見込み。ＥＣＢは、９月の理事会で政策金利
を0.25％ポイント引き上げ。インフレ加速が見込ま

れるなか、年内にも追加利上げを行う見通し。
政治面からも金利上昇圧力が増大。フランスでは、
来年度予算案で財政赤字の対ＧＤＰ比がＥＵ
目標を超えることが示され、長期金利の対独スプレッ
ドが急上昇。スペインでは、住宅価格の高騰対策が
否決され、政府が議会を解散。現時点の世論調査
では、11月の総選挙後に極右勢力が政権入りする
可能性が高く、拡張的な財政運営への警戒が増大。

◆個人消費は先行き減速へ
足元の英国景気は堅調。８月の小売売上数量
は前月比＋0.5%と、高めの伸び。販売チャネル別
にみると、無店舗販売が持ち直したほか、百貨店な
ど非専門店の販売も増加。
もっとも、先行きの個人消費は減速する見通し。イ
ンフレの加速が主因。８月の消費者物価は前年比
＋3.1％と、前月から伸びが拡大。電力・ガス市場
局（Ofgem）は、足元の資源高を踏まえて、
2027年１～３月期にかけて、エネルギー価格の上
限を段階的に引き上げる見込み。これにより、インフ
レ率は前年比３％台後半まで上昇すると予想。
◆ＡＩ需要が企業の生産活動を下支え
足元の生産活動は、業種によって二極化。ＧＤ
Ｐに占めるシェアが大きいサービス業では、消費者向
けが低調である一方、企業向けは堅調。旺盛なＡ
Ｉ需要を背景に、ＩＴ関連の情報サービス業などの
事業活動が活発化。製造業でも、コンピュータ・電
子製品・光学機械などＡＩ関連の生産が拡大。

先行きの生産活動は底堅く推移する見通し。個
人消費の減速を背景に消費者向けサービス業は伸
び悩む一方、ＡＩ関連の企業向けサービス業や製
造業の生産は今後も拡大すると予想。英国の民間
ＡＩ投資額は米国や中国に次ぐ規模であり、フラン
スやドイツなど大陸欧州諸国よりもＡＩ需要による
生産押し上げ効果が大きくなる公算。
英国景気概況：家計部門は減速も、ＡＩ需要が下支え

(株)日本総合研究所 欧州経済展望 2026年10月
◆健康問題と雇用悪化を背景にニートが増加
英国では、ニート（就業せず、教育・職業訓練も
受けていない16～24歳の若者）の数が近年増加
し、2026年４～６月期には約100万人に。その背
景として、次の２点が指摘可能。
第１に、健康問題を抱える若者の増加。就労に
支障をきたすほどの健康問題を抱えるニートの割合
は、2015年の26％から2025年に44％へ上昇。メ
ンタルヘルスの不調が主要な健康問題に。
第２に、若年層の雇用環境悪化。16～24歳の
失業率は、足元でコロナ禍のピークを上回る水準に
上昇。企業は採用を抑制しており、小売業や宿泊・
飲食サービス業といった、従来若年層が多く就労し
ていた産業の欠員は減少。加えて、政府による最低
賃金の引き上げを受けて、一部の企業では若年層
を採用する意欲が弱まった可能性も。
◆ニートの増加は経済・財政を圧迫
ニートの増加は、英国の経済・財政に悪影響。政
府の委託を受けてニート問題を調査した「ミルバー

ン・レポート」の中間報告は、ニートが就業していない
ことによるＧＤＰの損失が年間380億ポンド（約
7.9兆円）、それにより得られなかった税収や追加の
福祉支出などの合計が年間66億ポンド（約1.4兆
円）にのぼると試算。
ニートの数が今後も増加し、無業状態が長期化す
れば、英国の成長力が低下し、税収減や福祉支出
の増加を通じて財政のさらなる悪化につながるリスク。

(株)日本総合研究所 欧州経済展望 2026年10月5
欧州経済見通し：ユーロ圏・英国ともに当面は減速
欧州各国の経済・物価見通し
【ユーロ相場見通し】
◆緩やかなユーロ安基調で推移する見通し
９月のユーロドル相場は、上旬に1.16ドル台前半を中
心に横ばい圏で推移。ユーロ圏のインフレ率が前月から上
昇した一方、米雇用統計が市場予想を上回り、ＥＣＢ・
ＦＲＢともに早期利上げ観測が強まったことが背景。その
後、10月上旬にかけて、一時1.11ドル台半ばへユーロ
安・ドル高が進行。堅調な米経済指標がドル高圧力となっ
たほか、フランスの財政不安がユーロ安圧力に。
先行きのユーロドル相場は、緩やかなユーロ安基調で推
移する見通し。当面はフランスの財政不安が重石となるほ
か、ＥＣＢは12月の追加利上げ後、来春にかけて政策
金利を据え置く一方、ＦＲＢは追加利上げを実施すると
みられることが背景。もっとも、来年半ば以降は利上げが打
ち止めとなり、横ばい圏で推移する見込み。
＜ユーロ圏＞
◆来年初にかけて減速、その後持ち直し

ユーロ圏の景気は、来年初にかけて減速する見通
し。中東危機による物価や金利の上昇が内需を下
押しする見込み。ただし、南欧諸国を中心とする良
好な雇用環境が個人消費を下支えするほか、世界
的なＡＩ需要の高まりが企業の生産活動を押し上
げることで、景気の底割れは回避。ドイツの政府支
出や、復興基金を原資とする各国の公共投資の増
加も景気を下支えすると予想。インフレ圧力の緩和
を受けて、来春以降、景気は持ち直しへ。
先行きのリスクは、中東危機の長期化。原油・天
然ガス価格が高止まりする場合、インフレが想定以
上に長引くことで、石油・ガスへのエネルギー依存度
が高い南欧諸国を中心に個人消費が大きく落ち込
む恐れ。ＥＣＢが複数回の利上げを行い、ユーロ圏
全体の内需が一段と下押しされる可能性も。
＜英国＞
◆家計部門を中心に、景気は減速へ
英国の景気は、減速する見通し。世界的なＡＩ

## Document 725: 油价延续下跌：美国原油库存连续第二周增加，产量创历史新高，市场定价转向供需

Source PDF: all report pdf/油价延续下跌：美国原油库存连续第二周增加，产量创历史新高，市场定价转向供需.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-18
Pages: 4
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

截⾄ 11 ⽉ 13 ⽇当周，美国原油库存增加 338 万桶，超出市场预期的 270 万桶，汽油库存更激
增 519 万桶，创下今年 1 ⽉以来最⼤单周增幅。 WTI 原油在数据公布后持续下跌，抹去此前
中东油轮遭袭带来的盘中涨幅，市场定价逻辑正从 " 地缘⻛险溢价 " 转向 " 供需基本⾯ " 。
摘要：
美国原油库存连续两周攀升、产量刷新历史纪录，叠加全球需求前景持续⾛弱，国际油价承压
下⾏，中东局势对油价的提振效应明显衰退。市场正在经历⼀场定价逻辑的深层切换 —— 从 " 地
缘⻛险溢价 " 转向 " 供需基本⾯ " 。
周三公布的美国能源信息署（ EIA ）官⽅数据显⽰，截⾄ 11 ⽉ 13 ⽇当周，美国原油库存增加 338
万桶，超出市场预期的 270 万桶，汽油库存更激增 519 万桶，创下今年 1 ⽉以来最⼤单周增幅。
与此同时，美国原油产量升⾄历史新⾼， WTI 原油在数据公布后进⼀步扩⼤跌幅，此前凭借中东
油轮遭袭消息提振的盘中涨幅遭到悉数抹去。周三 WTI 原油期货下跌 3.33% 。

荷兰国际集团（ ING ）分析师表⽰： " 霍尔⽊兹海峡的进⼀步中断依然是重要的上⾏⻛险，但欧
佩克 + 产量上升、俄罗斯出⼝韧性犹存以及美国库存持续累积，共同勾勒出⼀幅供应趋于宽松的
图景。 " 该⾏预计，随着供应持续恢复，油市供需平衡将进⼀步松弛，油价持续反弹的空间因此
受限。
库存全⾯累积，汽油创阶段新⾼
官⽅数据与⾏业机构美国⽯油学会（ API ）此前公布的数据基本吻合，印证了供应端的持续宽松
态势。
API 数据显⽰，同期原油库存增加 530 万桶，汽油库存增加 510 万桶，馏分油库存增加 35.1 万
桶；⽽库欣地区库存则下降 170 万桶。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

EIA 官⽅数据中，库欣库存下降约 121 万桶，接近 " 罐底 " 警戒⽔位，馏分油库存增加 157 万桶。尽
管如此，美国馏分油总库存仍处于同期历史最低⽔平。
战略⽯油储备（ SPR ）同期再度⼤幅下降，减少约 317 万桶，为今年 8 ⽉以来最⼤单周降幅，当
前储备规模距历史最低点仅剩约 600 万桶。
美国原油产量本周升⾄历史新⾼，钻井平台数量持续攀升是产量增⻓的重要⽀撑。供应端的量
增与库存的同步累积，共同加剧了市场对供应过剩的担忧。

### Page 3

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。
地缘冲击溢价退潮，市场⽬光转向供需
过去两个交易⽇的价格⾛势，清晰勾勒出油市定价逻辑的转变。
周⼀，尽管伊朗袭击了美军在巴林的基地并宣称在霍尔⽊兹海峡发动更多⽔雷打击，布伦特原
油仍下跌逾 3%—— 导⽕索是霍尔⽊兹油流恢复信号所引发的供应过剩预期升温。
IEA 最新预测显⽰，2026 年第四季度全球⽯油供应将超出需求 170 万桶 / ⽇，全球可观测库存在 6
⽉后⾸次出现⽉度增⻓，累计增加 5200 万桶。
IEA 同时预计，2026 年全球⽯油需求将同⽐下降 270 万桶 / ⽇，降⾄每⽇ 1.023 亿桶。需求端的疲
软并⾮纯粹的周期性回落 ——130 美元⾼油价时期被压缩的消费需求，不会因布伦特跌回 90 美元
低位⽽⾃动复苏。
即便明年需求有所反弹，主要驱动⼒也集中于⽯化领域，⾼油价、电动化进程加速以及全球⻋

队燃油效率提升，将持续压制道路交通燃料需求的回升空间。
空头激进押注，短线挤仓⻛险不容忽视
然⽽，当前市场的悲观预期或已⾛在基本⾯前头。
布伦特原油价格仍较战前⽔平⾼出约 22 美元 / 桶，意味着地缘⻛险溢价并未完全消散。伊拉克⽬
前仍借道叙利亚陆路运油，表明其出⼝尚未恢复⾄战前⽔平。船舶追踪机构路孚特（ LSEG ）亦
警告称，霍尔⽊兹航运流量的正常化 " 在当前条件下仍不可能实现 " 。
布伦特原油本⽉已累计下跌 21% ，空头仓位⼤幅积累。在此背景下，⼀旦霍尔⽊兹海峡传出油
轮确认沉没的消息，触发的空头回补⼒度可能远超基本⾯所能⽀撑的幅度。
需求侧的结构性压⼒是缓慢演进的⻓期⼒量，供应侧则更多受制于突发新闻的短期冲击。当前
市场的天平已明显倒向需求逻辑，但押注霍尔⽊兹局势的交易者，历史上并⾮没有翻⻋的前⻋
之鉴。

## Document 731: 消費者物価指数（全国・2026年10月） ～予想下振れも、先行きの物価再加速シナリオは変わらず～

Source PDF: all report pdf/消費者物価指数（全国・2026年10月） ～予想下振れも、先行きの物価再加速シナリオは変わらず～.pdf
Report source: DLRI
Date: 2026-11-20
Pages: 4
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Economic Indicators

2026年 11月20日

1/4 
消費者物価指数（全国・2026年 10月） 
～予想下振れも、先行きの物価再加速シナリオは変わらず～ 
経済調査部 シニアエグゼクティブエコノミスト 新家義貴 
（℡：050-5474-7490）

エネルギー主導で小幅加速、予想対比ではやや弱め 
本日総務省から発表された 26年 10月の全国消費者物価指数では、生鮮食品を除く総合（ＣＰ
Ｉコア）が前年比＋2.2％と、９月の＋2.1％から上昇率が 0.1％Pt拡大した。２ヵ月連続で２％台
となったものの、市場予想の＋2.3％はやや下回った。エネルギー価格の上昇が全体を押し上げた
一方、米価格の大幅下落を主因に食料品の伸びが鈍化したことが、上昇率を抑える要因となっ
た。 
また、日銀版コア（生鮮食品及びエネルギーを除く総合）も前年比＋2.2％と、9月の＋2.4％か
ら伸びが縮小している。９月には幅広い品目で物価上昇率が加速し、先行きの物価上振れ懸念が
強まっていたが、10月はそうした懸念を一段と高めるほどの結果にはならなかった。予想対比で
やや弱めの内容だったと評価できる。 
今月のＣＰＩを押し上げたのは電気・ガス代の上昇である。電気・都市ガス代は前年比▲1.9％
から＋2.3％へ上昇率が拡大し、ＣＰＩコアへの寄与度も▲0.09％Ptから＋0.11％Ptへ高まった。
一方、生鮮食品を除く食料は前年比＋2.9％から＋2.2％へ鈍化し、寄与度も＋0.71％Ptから＋
0.55％Ptへ低下している。米類の寄与度は前年比▲0.22％Ptから▲0.33％Ptへ低下しており、食
